Method: CACTUS的核心方法：1）将标签一致的语义对（label-consistent semantic pairs）转换为目标导向的表示偏移（target-directed representation shifts）；2）使用掩码引导（mask-guided）、模态特定的算子（modality-specific operators）隔离触发效应；3）将触发效应跨样本耦合；4）在对等聚合前，将偏移以反事实方式（counterfactually）施加到干净的非目标嵌入上。

Result: 在九种聚合规则下、四种模态（语音、文本、表格、图像）的实验表明：30%恶意节点时，CACTUS在Speech Commands数据集上达到51.2%的九规则平均ASR；在四种模态中的三种上取得最高的九规则平均攻击成功率；敏感性分析显示ASR随网络拓扑变化，并随恶意节点比例增加而上升。

Conclusion: 结果表明CACTUS能够通过重复的DFL聚合传播后门攻击，证明了拓扑感知的语义后门攻击在去中心化联邦学习环境中的有效性和持久性。

Abstract: Semantic triggers in federated learning (FL) can be less conspicuous than synthetic patches, but sample-dependent placement may weaken backdoor implantation across aggregation rounds. This challenge is compounded in decentralized FL (DFL), where topology-dependent peer aggregation repeatedly mixes local models. CACTUS converts label-consistent semantic pairs into target-directed representation shifts. Mask-guided, modality-specific operators isolate trigger effects, couple them across samples, and apply the shifts counterfactually to clean non-target embeddings before peer aggregation. Experiments cover speech, text, tabular, and image tasks under nine aggregation rules. With 30\% malicious nodes, CACTUS reaches a nine-rule mean attack success rate (ASR) of 51.2\% on Speech Commands and the highest nine-rule mean ASR among evaluated attacks on three of four modalities. Sensitivity analyses show that ASR varies with network topology and increases with the malicious-node ratio. These results indicate that CACTUS can propagate backdoors through repeated DFL aggregation.

</details>


### [299] [Scalable Kronecker-Fisher Approximation: Efficient Hessian Analysis for Billion-Parameter Language Models Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02451)
*Viacheslav Yusupov,Daria Cherniuk,Evgeny Frolov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于克罗内克积的可扩展近似方法来分析十亿级参数网络的跨层Fisher信息，无需存储完整Fisher矩阵即可进行Hessian分析，并揭示value投影层是最脆弱、跨层相关性最强的组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经网络中计算和存储完整Fisher信息矩阵是不可行的，但理解参数的二阶敏感性（Hessian）对模型压缩、脆弱性分析和优化至关重要。需要一个可扩展的近似方法来捕获跨层交互。

Method: 采用克罗内克积（Kronecker）近似来估计Fisher信息，避免存储完整Fisher矩阵，同时保持跨层交互信息的捕获能力，实现对十亿级参数网络的实用化Hessian分析。

Result: 发现跨多种模型家族一致性的脆弱性模式：value投影层对性能影响最大且跨层相关性最强，而其他组件表现架构特有行为。通过量化、稀疏化、层间损坏及损坏后微调等实验验证，该近似与性能退化及恢复高度相关。

Conclusion: 提供了一个实用的、有理论基础的框架来识别大型模型中的脆弱组件，为引导压缩和优化策略（如混合精度分配、逐层稀疏度、跨层及权重组的自适应低秩分解）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a scalable Kronecker-based approximation that captures cross-layer interactions without storing the entire Fisher matrix, enabling practical Hessian analysis for billion-parameter networks where full computation is infeasible. Our approach reveals consistent vulnerability patterns: value projection layers exhibit the highest sensitivity and strongest cross-layer correlations across multiple model families, while other components exhibit architecture-specific behaviors. Through extensive experiments on quantization, sparsification, inter-layer corruption, and post-corruption fine-tuning, we demonstrate that our approximation strongly correlates with both performance degradation and recovery. Our framework provides a practical, theoretically grounded tool for identifying fragile components in large models, opening new avenues for guided compression and optimization strategies, such as mixed-precision allocation, layer-wise sparsity, and adaptive low-rank decomposition across layers and even individual weight groups.

</details>


### [300] [DeepAffinity: Long-Term Aspect Preference Prediction in eCommerce using Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02468)
*Yotam Eshel,Guy Hadad,Guy Feigenblat,Yuri M. Brovman,Matt Gearhart,Bracha Shapira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"Aspect Affinity"的任务，即预测电商用户对产品属性（如品牌、尺寸、颜色）的偏好。作者提出了DeepAffinity框架，利用小型语言模型（SLMs）配合结构化提示和专用预测头进行微调，用于时序预测用户的未来属性选择。实验表明DeepAffinity优于标准生成式微调方法，且未定制调优的通用开源LLM在该任务上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台需要超越当前会话的粒度级用户偏好理解，以提升推荐、搜索和营销的个性化水平。传统的方面级偏好预测未被充分建模为长期时序任务，通用LLM也缺乏对细微行为的建模能力，需要专门的方法来预测用户在品牌、尺寸、颜色等产品属性上的未来选择。

Method: 将Aspect Affinity建模为时序预测任务，基于用户按时间排序的交互历史预测未来的方面选择。提出DeepAffinity框架，采用结构化提示的小型语言模型（SLMs），并设计专用的预测头进行任务微调，以替代标准的生成式微调方案。

Result: DeepAffinity在Aspect Affinity任务上优于标准生成式微调方法；未经任务定制调优的通用开源LLM表现差；在大型跨国电商平台上DeepAffinity有效提升了推荐质量。

Conclusion: DeepAffinity证明了结构化提示与专用预测头结合的小型语言模型是建模电商方面级用户偏好的有效方法，在细粒度个性化推荐方面优于通用大语言模型的默认能力，并已在大规模电商场景中验证了实际业务价值。

Abstract: We explore predicting eCommerce user preferences for product aspects such as brand, size, and color - a task we define as Aspect Affinity. Solving this task improves customer understanding and enables fine-grained personalization in recommendation, search, and marketing. We frame Aspect Affinity as a temporal prediction task: forecasting a users future aspect choices from their time-ordered interaction history, capturing long-term preferences that evolve beyond the current session. To this end, we propose DeepAffinity, which leverages Small Language Models (SLMs) with structured prompts and specialized prediction heads fine-tuned for this task. We show DeepAffinity outperforms standard generative fine-tuning methods, while general-purpose open-source LLMs perform poorly without task-specific tuning, highlighting their limits in modeling nuanced behavior. Finally, DeepAffinity enhances recommendation quality on a large-scale multinational eCommerce platform.

</details>


### [301] [RINSE: Robust Target-Time Normality Estimation for Zero-Shot Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02497)
*Taufikur Rahman Fuad,Md Abrar Jahin,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RINSE（鲁棒迭代正态性自估计）框架，一种梯度无关的目标时序框架，用于零样本图异常检测。该框架保持源模型固定，通过迭代地从目标图中估计目标正态性、表征校准和证据可靠性，利用低残差目标节点构建裁剪后的目标感知正态性模型，并通过可靠性门控的秩融合和编码器集成组合互补的异常证据。在八个未见目标图上取得最高平均AUPRC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本图异常检测面临跨域（源域到目标域）的域偏移问题，导致源数据上学到的正态性概念在未见的目标图上不可靠，而现有方法缺乏在无标签、无梯度的目标时序中进行有效自适应的机制。

Method: RINSE采用梯度无关的目标时序框架：1）保持源训练检测器固定；2）依次从目标图中估计目标正态性、表征校准和证据可靠性；3）识别低残差的目标节点子集以构建裁剪后的目标感知正态性模型；4）通过可靠性门控的秩融合与编码器集成来组合互补的异常检测证据。

Result: 在八个未见目标图上，RINSE在两种独立的预处理协议下均取得所有评估方法中最高的平均AUPRC；模块消融实验和敏感性分析进一步验证了整体设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，鲁棒的目标时序估计是实现无需目标标签、梯度和逐目标调参的通用图异常检测的实用途径，支持了目标时序自适应方法在零样本图异常检测中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Zero-shot graph anomaly detection seeks to deploy a detector trained on source graphs to unseen, unlabeled targets, yet domain shift can make source-derived notions of normality unreliable. We introduce RINSE (Robust Iterative Normality Self-Estimation), a gradient-free target-time framework that keeps the source-trained detector fixed while sequentially estimating target normality, representation calibration, and evidence reliability from the target graph. Its core idea is to identify a reliable subset of low-residual target nodes, use them to construct a trimmed target-aware normality model, and combine complementary anomaly evidence through reliability-gated rank fusion and encoder ensembling. Across eight unseen target graphs, RINSE achieves the highest average AUPRC among the evaluated methods under two separate preprocessing protocols, while block ablations and sensitivity analyses support the combined design. These results support robust target-time estimation as a practical approach to generalist graph anomaly detection without target labels, gradients, or per-target tuning.

</details>


### [302] [Rethinking the Teacher-Student Framework for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02507)
*Damian Sójka,Marc Masana,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了测试时适应（TTA）中广泛采用的教师-学生框架，揭示了即使使用指数移动平均（EMA）更新教师权重，在长序列上仍会产生错误累积。作者提出使用一个不更新权重的"顽固教师"（intransigent teacher），发现这一简单改动能在多种数据集和长场景下显著提升TTA性能，并增强对超参数变化的鲁棒性，且可无缝应用于包括语义分割在内的各种架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管教师-学生框架被广泛用于缓解TTA中的错误累积，但其长期稳定性往往被想当然地接受。作者发现仅考察常用短序列时EMA教师的表现掩盖了深层的错误累积问题，因此需要重新审视教师权重更新策略以解决长序列下的稳定性问题。

Method: 1) 分析教师-学生框架内的稳定性-可塑性权衡（stability-plasticity trade-off）；2) 指出EMA教师权重更新策略在长序列下仍存在错误累积；3) 提出使用不更新权重的顽固教师（intransigent teacher）替代EMA教师；4) 在多种数据集、长场景、不同架构及语义分割等多个实验设置中验证该方法。

Result: 简单使用顽固教师即可显著提升TTA方法在更长场景下的性能，同时增强了对超参数的鲁棒性，并且该方法可无缝应用于各种架构和语义分割等不同实验设置。

Conclusion: 固定教师权重（顽固教师）是一种比EMA老师更稳定有效的策略，能够帮助TTA方法在长序列部署中克服错误累积问题，且并不牺牲可塑性。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) has recently emerged as a promising strategy that allows the adaptation of pre-trained models to changing data distributions at deployment time, without access to any labels. To mitigate error accumulation, researchers have widely adopted the teacher-student framework, though its long-term stability is often taken for granted. In this work, we challenge the common strategy of setting the teacher weights to an exponential moving average of the student by showing that error accumulation still occurs, although it is mostly apparent on longer sequences compared to those commonly utilized. We analyze the stability-plasticity trade-off within the teacher-student framework and propose to use an intransigent teacher that does not update its weights. Surprisingly, we show that this simple change allows TTA methods to significantly improve their performance on multiple datasets with longer scenarios and result in increased robustness to changes in hyperparameters. Finally, we show that those changes can be seamlessly and effectively applied to various architectures and experimental setups, including semantic segmentation. The code is available at https://github.com/dmn-sjk/intransigent_teacher.

</details>


### [303] [Spectral Initialization and Scheduled Graph Smoothness for Uncertain Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02519)
*Md Abrar Jahin,Taufikur Rahman Fuad,Jay Pujara,Craig A. Knoblock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUEST方法通过使用置信度加权图拉普拉斯的谱特征初始化实体嵌入，并引入无偏小批量Dirichlet能量正则化器，在不增加任何可训练参数的情况下，提升不确定知识图谱（UKG）补全的预测精度、训练稳定性与检查点可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定知识图谱中的大多数三元组缺乏观测到的置信度分数，现有半监督方法在生成伪标签时忽略置信度加权图所蕴含的全局社区与枢纽结构，仅用非加权方式初始化实体嵌入，导致早期训练缺乏结构先验，影响UKG补全效果。

Method: QUEST提出两点改进：(1) 利用置信度加权图拉普拉斯的最小非平凡特征向量初始化实体嵌入，在训练前融入社区与枢纽结构；(2) 引入无偏的小批量Dirichlet能量正则化器，在训练早期阶段施加图结构一致性约束。该方法不增加任何可训练参数，直接嵌入标准置信度分布学习流程。

Result: 在两个UKG数据集上，QUEST在八个指标-数据集组合中的六个上超越现有方法，在剩余两个上追平此前最佳表现。同时，QUEST消除了在稠密图上出现的训练不稳定尖峰（instability spike），表明谱结构先验与Dirichlet正则化的组合有效。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，将谱结构先验与图Dirichlet能量正则化相结合，能够在不增加模型复杂度的情况下，同时提升UKG补全的准确性、训练稳定性以及检查点可靠性。

Abstract: Uncertain knowledge graphs (UKGs) extend knowledge graphs by assigning each triple a continuous confidence score. Since most possible triples lack observed confidences, recent methods rely on semi-supervised learning to generate pseudo-labels. These methods initialize entity embeddings without using the confidence-weighted graph, discarding its global community and hub structure. We introduce QUEST, which adds no trainable parameters to the standard confidence-distribution learning pipeline. First, QUEST initializes entity embeddings using the smallest non-trivial eigenvectors of the confidence-weighted graph Laplacian, incorporating community and hub structure before training. Second, QUEST applies an unbiased mini-batch Dirichlet energy regularizer to enforce early-stage structural consistency. On two UKG datasets, QUEST improves confidence prediction and link prediction on six of eight metric-dataset pairs over prior methods and matches the previous best on the remaining two, while removing the instability spike observed on dense graphs. These results indicate that spectral structural priors combined with a graph Dirichlet energy regularizer improve accuracy, training stability, and checkpoint reliability in UKG completion.

</details>


### [304] [A Comparative Study of Graph Representations for GNN-Based Power Grid Control in L2RPN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02538)
*Adrian Degenkolb,Qiong Huang,Benjamin Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对电力网络控制中深度强化学习的图构建方式进行了受控对比实验，比较了物理拓扑、电气灵敏度及混合变体等图表示方法，发现图复杂度与任务粒度匹配比单纯追求表示丰富性更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图构建是深度强化学习中关键的但在电力网络控制领域尚未被充分研究的设计选择，缺乏系统性的对照实验来指导这一决策。

Method: 在L2RPN环境中对多种图表示（物理拓扑图、电气灵敏度图以及混合变体）进行受控实验对比，用于拓扑控制任务。

Result: 图复杂度与任务粒度的匹配程度比最大化表示丰富性更能决定模型性能；该发现强调了在大规模场景中进行受控表示研究的重要性。

Conclusion: 在电力网络深度强化学习的图表示选择中，应根据具体任务粒度调整图复杂度，而非盲目追求信息更丰富的图结构；需要更大规模、更系统的受控研究来验证和拓展这一结论。

Abstract: Graph construction is a critical but underexamined design choice in deep reinforcement learning for power grid control. We present a controlled experimental comparison of different graph representations, including physical topology, electrical-sensitivity, and hybrid variants for topology control in the Learning to Run a Power Network (L2RPN) environment. Our findings indicate that matching graph complexity to task granularity is more important than maximizing representational richness, and highlight the importance of controlled representation studies at scale.

</details>


### [305] [TrajMind: Chaining Role-Specialized LoRAs for Fast-and-Slow Collective Trajectory Anomaly Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02540)
*Jiahao Wu,Zhenqun Yang,Chen Jason Zhang,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajMind框架，采用"快-慢"双路径架构从城市轨迹中诊断集体异常。慢路径(TrajMinds-low)通过画布分类、类型条件定位和可执行验证生成有结构、有证据支持的诊断；快路径(TrajMind-fast)以单一文本通道实现低延迟异常筛查。实验表明TrajMind在异常类型识别上超过最强基线15.3个百分点，定位上超过13.8个百分点，跨城市泛化性能保持稳定，且快路径将延迟降低41.1%并维持至少93.5%的二分类平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集体异常检测方法要么仅产生分数/标签而缺乏可验证的语义信息，要么依赖视觉-语言管道提供丰富语义但无法实现低延迟实时监控。核心挑战在于如何在无需对每个监控窗口运行完整诊断管道的情况下，从源轨迹中识别集体模式并恢复确切的what-who-where-when事件细节。

Method: 提出TrajMind框架，采用fast-and-slow双路径设计：(1) 慢路径TrajMinds-low通过画布式异常类型分类、基于序列化轨迹的类型条件定位以及可执行验证三个链式步骤，生成结构化且有证据支持的诊断结果；(2) 快路径TrajMind-fast在单个纯文本通道中完成每个时间窗口的筛查，输出高效的结构化警报；(3) 框架在单一冻结的视觉-语言骨干上切换三个角色专用的LoRA适配器，实现始终在线的筛查与按需诊断的分离。

Result: TrajMinds-low在异常类型识别上比最强基线提升至少15.3个百分点，在定位上提升至少13.8个百分点；这些性能优势在跨城市迁移场景下依然保持。TrajMind-fast将延迟降低41.1%，同时保持至少93.5%的二分类平衡准确率。

Conclusion: TrajMind通过分离即时筛查与按需诊断，在仅对一个冻结的视觉-语言骨干切换三个角色专用LoRA适配器的条件下，同时实现了跨城市的准确、有证据支持的诊断与高效的前线监控，有效解决了集体异常检测中诊断可验证性与监控低延迟之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Diagnosing collective anomalies from urban trajectories is increasingly important for traffic governance, as it reveals what happened, who was involved, and where and when the event occurred. Existing detectors efficiently produce scores or labels, whereas vision--language pipelines provide richer semantics; neither couples verifiable diagnosis with low-latency monitoring. The central challenge is to recognize collective patterns and recover exact event details from the source trajectories without running the full diagnostic pipeline for every monitored window. We therefore separate always-on screening from on-demand diagnosis: screening raises alerts, while diagnosis releases only source-verified what--who--where--when records. We present TrajMind, a fast-and-slow framework that switches three role-specialized LoRA adapters over one frozen vision--language backbone. Its slow path, \textit{TrajMind$_{\text{slow}}$}, chains canvas-based typing, type-conditioned localization over serialized trajectories, and executable verification, yielding structured, evidence-backed diagnoses. Additionally, the fast path, \textit{TrajMind$_{\text{fast}}$}, screens each window in a single text-only pass, delivering efficient structured alerts. Extensive experiments show that, TrajMind$_{\mathrm{slow}}$ outperforms the strongest baselines by at least $15.3$ percentage points in anomaly typing and $13.8$ percentage points in localization. These gains persist under cross-city transfer, and TrajMind$_{\mathrm{fast}}$ reduces latency by $41.1\%$ and maintains binary balanced accuracy of at least $93.5\%$. Together, TrajMind delivers accurate, evidence-backed diagnoses across cities and efficient front-line monitoring.

</details>


### [306] [Learn from Whoever Is Right: Answer-Verified Multi-Teacher Distillation for Multi-Domain LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02548)
*Xixiang He,Xingming Li,Baiqi Wu,Qiyao Sun,Xuanyu Ji,Ao Cheng,Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-SDPO（多教师自蒸馏策略优化），一种在线策略蒸馏方法，通过三个组件（自锚点、答案验证资格、特权蒸馏）将多个冻结教师的能力统一到一个学生模型中，实现按样本而非按领域选择可靠教师。在三个模型家族的五个学生上验证，其中Qwen3-8B最弱领域提升14.79分，领域差距缩小74.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法按领域标签路由样本到匹配的教师，但领域专长只在平均意义上成立：匹配的教师并非对每个样本都正确，而其他领域的教师有时反而正确。因此可靠教师应在样本级别而非领域级别识别。

Method: MT-SDPO包含三个组件：(1) 自锚点——用自身组内的正确rollout监督当前rollout；(2) 答案验证资格——教师只有在其自身答案通过验证器后才可监督该样本；(3) 特权蒸馏——将锚点与所有验证通过的反馈合并到同一上下文中，由EMA自教师读取而学生不可见，从而部署时保持单一策略。

Result: 在三个模型家族的五个学生上验证，MT-SDPO使Qwen3-8B最弱领域提升14.79分，领域差距缩小74.7%，优于按领域各部署一个匹配教师的方式。

Conclusion: 应依据验证过的可靠性而非领域归属来决定谁来教学，这为多教师蒸馏提供了更优范式。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) rely on reinforcement learning to build strong capabilities in individual domains, but integrating those capabilities into a single deployable model remains challenging. By routing each sample to the teacher whose domain matches it, existing approaches let a domain label decide which teacher provides supervision. However, domain expertise holds only on average: the matched teacher is not always correct on a given sample, while a teacher from another domain sometimes is. The reliable teacher therefore has to be identified per sample, not per domain. In this paper, we introduce Multi-Teacher Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (MT-SDPO), an on-policy distillation method that unifies several frozen teachers into one student model. MT-SDPO consists of three components: (1) self-anchors, where a rollout is supervised by a correct rollout from its own group; (2) answer-verified eligibility, where a teacher may supervise a sample only if its own answer passes a verifier; and (3) privileged distillation, which merges the anchor and all verified feedback into one context that an exponential moving average self-teacher reads and the student does not, thereby keeping one policy at deployment. Across five students from three model families, MT-SDPO lifts the weakest domain of Qwen3-8B by 14.79 points and narrows its domain gap by 74.7%, a better balance than serving one matched teacher per domain. Verified reliability, not domain membership, should decide who teaches. Code is available at https://github.com/hexixiang/MT-SDPO.

</details>


### [307] [ProbeMatchDTI: Probe-Driven Multi-Scale Biochemical Pattern Matching for Drug-Target Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02549)
*Quan Hao,Mengyue Fan,Zifan Dong,Youru Li,Jianduo Zhao,Lechuan Xu,Hao Zhang,Fei Xia,Jigang Wang,Chong Qiu,Liguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProbeMatchDTI是一个面向药物-靶点相互作用（DTI）预测的模式探针驱动框架，包含IterProbe和BindingProbe两个模块，通过显式保留上下文状态并以可学习探针选择弱生化模式，缓解传统被动特征聚合对弱但结合相关信号的抑制问题，在BindingDB和DrugBank上分别提升2.0%和0.5%的AUC-ROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生化表征学习方法在DTI预测中采用被动特征聚合，倾向于强化主导分子模式而抑制弱但仍与结合相关的信号（如官能团、残基上下文模式），限制了跨尺度的生化对应关系建模。

Method: 提出ProbeMatchDTI框架，包含两个关键模块：1) IterProbe在细化过程中显式保留上下文状态，使用可学习的探针在跨实体匹配前于每个位置选择这些状态，从而保留弱生化模式并强化官能团、局部基序与分子支架间的关联；2) BindingProbe在局部生化单元和整体配对两个层面刻画药物-蛋白质互补性，联合建模细粒度交互与多尺度对应关系，同时保留较弱的结合相关关联。此外，将预测结果连接到证据引导的下游药物发现工作流。

Result: 大量实验表明ProbeMatchDTI在BindingDB上AUC-ROC提高2.0%，在DrugBank上提高0.5%；特征层面的模式分析进一步刻画了其探针驱动的跨尺度生化模式匹配行为。

Conclusion: ProbeMatchDTI通过探针驱动的模式选择机制有效提升了DTI预测性能，并能与下游药物发现工作流连接，为候选药物优化和验证规划提供实用价值。

Abstract: Drug-target interaction (DTI) prediction is an important task in AI-driven drug discovery. Although recent biochemical representation learning methods have improved DTI prediction, their passive feature aggregation tends to favor dominant molecular patterns while suppressing weak yet binding-relevant signals, such as functional groups and residue-context patterns, limiting the modeling of multi-scale biochemical correspondences. To address this issue, we propose ProbeMatchDTI, a pattern-probe-driven framework comprising IterProbe and BindingProbe. IterProbe explicitly retains contextual states across refinement depths and uses learnable probes to select them at each position before cross-entity matching, thereby preserving weak biochemical patterns and strengthening associations among functional groups, local motifs, and molecular scaffolds. BindingProbe then characterizes cross-entity drug-protein complementarity at local biochemical-unit and whole-pair levels, jointly modeling fine-grained interactions and multi-scale correspondences while preserving weaker binding-relevant associations. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of ProbeMatchDTI, achieving 2.0% and 0.5% higher AUC-ROC on BindingDB and DrugBank, respectively. Feature-level pattern analyses further characterize its probe-driven behavior in cross-scale biochemical pattern matching. We further connect ProbeMatchDTI predictions with an evidence-guided downstream drug-discovery workflow, demonstrating their utility for candidate refinement and validation planning. Our code is available at https://github.com/developer-hq/ProbeMatchDTI

</details>


### [308] [Online Reinforcement Learning in the Met Office Unified Model through Distributed Model-Agent Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02566)
*Pritthijit Nath,Sebastian Schemm,Peter Haynes,Emily Shuckburgh,Mark Webb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将分布式强化学习（DDPG）智能体耦合到Met Office统一模型（UM）中，通过秩局部张量在70个垂直层共享权重，对模式倾向施加有界的位温修正。在十个氮化训练预报后，冻结策略被应用于无氮化推断，结果显示在+6小时时，Z500 MAE在六个纬度带中的四个有所降低（南、北热带分别减少45.8%和40.8%），MSLP误差在三个纬度带降低（最大减少27.3%），验证了分布式在线学习后接无氮化推断在数值天气预报中的可行性与潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习的订正须使其适应不断演变的模式状态，同时保持动力一致性和数值稳定性，才能有效补充数值天气预报。本研究旨在探索在真实全球预报模式中实现RL驱动的在线学习订正与推断的可行性。

Method: 将Met Office统一模式（UM）与基于秩局部张量的分布式RL智能体耦合：单一DDPG actor在每大气柱的70个垂直层共享权重，对模式倾向施加有界位温修正。训练阶段通过十个氮化预报将计算向UKMO业务分析氮化以获得反事实目标；评估阶段将冻结策略应用于无氮化预报中进行推断。

Result: 耦合工作流成功完成训练且评估案例数值稳定。与匹配的原生UM预报在+6小时相比，Z500 MAE在六个纬度带中的四个降低，其中南、北热带分别降低45.8%和40.8%；MSLP误差在三个纬度带降低，0-30°N最大降幅达27.3%。

Conclusion: 该单例实验证明了分布式在线学习后接无氮化推断具有显著潜力和可行性，为基于RL的偏差订正和参数化方案在业务系统中落地奠定了基础。

Abstract: Machine-learnt corrections can complement numerical weather prediction only if they adapt to the evolving model state while preserving dynamical consistency and numerical stability. To test this within a global forecasting model, we couple the Met Office (UKMO) Unified Model (UM) with distributed RL agents through rank-local tensors. A DDPG actor shares weights across the 70 vertical model levels of each atmospheric column and applies bounded potential-temperature corrections to the model tendencies. Across ten nudged training forecasts, nudging calculations towards the UKMO operational analysis provides an immediate counterfactual target. The frozen policy is then evaluated in a non-nudged forecast for inference. The coupled workflow successfully completes training and remains numerically stable in the evaluated case. Relative to a matched native UM forecast at +6 h, the learnt policy reduces Z$_{500}$ MAE in four of six latitude bands, including reductions of 45.8% and 40.8% in the northern and southern tropics. MSLP error too decreases in three bands, with a maximum reduction of 27.3% at 0-30°N. This single-case experiment demonstrates significant promise and feasibility of distributed online learning followed by non-nudged inference, laying the groundwork for RL-based bias correction and parametrisations within operational systems.

</details>


### [309] [Source Distribution Estimation by Posterior Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02622)
*Trung-Dung Hoang,Lisa M. Koch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过期望最大化（EM）框架解决仿真参数源分布估计（SDE）问题：E步基于当前源估计生成新仿真并训练摊销后验，M步将源分布重新拟合到观测数据下后验的平均。给出两种参数化（分离的源与后验流，或共享条件流），实验证明在三个基准任务上优于固定替代模型方法及其迭代变体，尤其在Lotka–Volterra任务中优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于仿真的科学常需要估计一个参数分布，使其前向推演能重现真实观测（即SDE问题）。现有方法依赖基于固定先验一次性训练的似然替代模型，其目标函数仅针对替代模型而非真实模拟器，导致在替代模型未训练过的参数区域可能失效。

Method: 采用期望最大化（EM）求解SDE：E步在来自当前源估计的新仿真上训练摊销后验；M步将源分布重新拟合到观测数据下后验的平均。提出两种参数化：(1) 分离的源流和后验流；(2) 单一共享的条件流。

Result: 在三个基准任务、宽泛与错误设定初始先验两种设置下评估，两种方法均优于现有固定替代模型方法及其迭代变体。在Lotka–Volterra任务中，所有基线数据空间C2ST不低于0.96，而该方法在四种初始先验设置中的三种达到0.64–0.68。

Conclusion: 基于EM的迭代刷新仿真与更新的方法能有效规避固定替代模型在未训练区域失效的问题，在SDE任务上取得了显著的精度提升。

Abstract: Simulation-based science often requires a distribution over simulator parameters whose push-forward reproduces a set of real observations: this is the source distribution estimation (SDE) problem. Existing methods fit the source against a likelihood surrogate trained once from a fixed proposal prior. Their objective is therefore stated only in terms of the surrogate instead of the true simulator, which may fail for inaccurate areas in parameter space where the surrogate was never trained. We instead solve SDE by expectation maximization: an E-step trains an amortized posterior on fresh simulations from the current source estimate, and an M-step refits the source to the average of that posterior over the observed data. We give two parameterizations, (1) separate source and posterior flows and (2) a single shared conditional flow. We evaluate our method on three benchmark tasks under both broad and misspecified initial priors. Both improve on existing fixed surrogate approaches and on iterated variants of each, most clearly on Lotka--Volterra, where no baseline falls below 0.96 data-space C2ST while our methods reach 0.64-0.68 in three of four initial-prior settings.

</details>


### [310] [Oracle, will I ever learn? A study of prediction convergence and complementarity across link prediction models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02638)
*Guillaume Méroué,Fabien Gandon,Pierre Monnin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了知识图谱链接预测模型中不同模型之间的互补性问题，通过"oracle"（对每个查询选择最佳预测的机制）来衡量模型间互补性所能达到的性能上限。研究发现不同模型确实捕获了互补的知识，但互补性随模型数量增加迅速饱和，存在一个即使是大量模型也无法解决的查询子集，揭示了当前链接预测模型的集体能力上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同链接预测模型（甚至同一模型的不同训练运行）对相同查询可能产生截然不同的预测结果，这表明模型对底层知识的捕获存在差异性。这引出一个基本问题：不同模型在多大程度上捕获了互补的知识，以及通过组合它们能恢复多少被遗漏的知识？回答这一问题对构建鲁棒的Web应用（如搜索、问答、推荐系统）具有重要意义。

Method: 提出通过"oracle"机制来度量模型互补性：对于每个查询，oracle从一组模型中选取最佳预测，从而提供模型组合可实现性能的上界。在多种架构和多个基准数据集上进行系统实验，比较单个模型性能与oracle性能之间的差距。

Result: 实验发现：（1）单个模型与oracle之间存在显著差距，表明不同模型确实捕获了互补的知识；（2）这种互补性随着模型数量的增加迅速饱和；（3）即使加入大量模型，仍存在一个持续无法解决的查询子集，即存在集体知识捕获的根本局限。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了模型互补性的潜力和当前链接预测模型的集体能力边界。虽然组合不同模型可以恢复部分互补知识，但互补性的快速饱和表明单纯依赖更多模型无法解决根本性问题，需要进一步研究以构建更鲁棒的Web应用。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have become an important source of structured knowledge for Web applications, including search, question answering, and recommender systems. In these applications, link prediction can serve either as a prediction task itself or as a means to enrich incomplete knowledge graphs for downstream tasks. Interestingly, different link prediction models, or even different training runs of the same model, can produce substantially different predictions for the same query. This suggests a variability in the capture of the underlying knowledge by models, thus raising a fundamental question: to what extent do different models capture complementary knowledge, and how much of this knowledge could be recovered by combining them? We propose to measure model complementarity through the performance of an oracle that, for each query, selects the best prediction among a considered set of models, hence providing an upper bound on the performance achievable through model combination. Across several architectures and benchmarks, we find a substantial gap between individual models and their oracle, revealing that different models capture complementary knowledge. Yet, this complementarity rapidly saturates as more models are added, leaving a persistent subset of queries unsolved even by a large number of models. These findings reveal both the potential of model complementarity and a fundamental limit to what current link prediction models can collectively recover; thereby highlighting the need for further research to build robust Web applications.

</details>


### [311] [Differentiable Electricity-Market Clearing for Gradient-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02646)
*Luca Mungo,Maarten P. Scholl,Arnau Quera-Bofarull*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电力市场清算建模为可微优化层，通过反向模式自动微分将规划成本梯度传播回数据中心选址方案，从而将大型数据中心选址规划变为可梯度搜索的优化问题。在合成双电力网络上进行50MW负载分配验证，梯度优化近乎精确恢复连续分配，最坏目标差距仅为最优与最差单站点成本差的2.3%和8.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据中心规划困难在于：有影响力的设施规模会改变其支付的电力市场价格，而价格由市场清算（每个运行条件下的约束优化问题）决定。传统模拟方法只能告知规划者某个候选方案的表现，却无法指导如何改进方案。

Method: 将市场清算视为可微优化层：每次前向传播求解市场均衡，反向模式自动微分将规划成本通过清算价格传播回规划变量（站点分配方案）。通过有限差分验证梯度正确性后，在36个运行状态下评估50MW数据中心负载在6个候选母线上的分配，考虑了每个活动站点的固定成本。

Result: 与穷举所有站点组合对比，梯度优化几乎精确恢复连续分配，最坏情况目标差距仅为最优与最差单站点成本差的2.3%和8.5%。其系统性错误具有启发性：当成本接近站点应关闭的阈值时，离散站点数量的平滑松弛表现为收缩站点而非关闭站点，导致离散转换延迟出现。

Conclusion: 可微市场清算将市场感知的数据中心规划转变为梯度可搜索的优化问题，为大规模设施选址提供了高效的自动化规划手段。

Abstract: Planning a large data center is difficult because a facility big enough to matter changes the electricity prices it will pay. Those prices are set by market clearing, a constrained optimization problem solved anew in every operating condition. However, simulating the market tells a planner how a candidate plan performs but not how to improve it. Here we treat market clearing as a differentiable optimization layer: each forward pass solves the market, and reverse-mode automatic differentiation propagates the planning cost back through the cleared prices to the plan. After validating these gradients against finite differences, we apply them to a concrete problem: allocating 50 MW of data-center load across six candidate buses in two synthetic networks, under a fixed cost per active site, evaluated over 36 operating states. Judged against exhaustive enumeration of all site combinations, gradient optimization recovers the continuous allocations almost exactly, with worst-case objective gaps of 2.3\% and 8.5\% of the cost difference between the best and worst single site. Its one systematic error is instructive: near the costs at which a site should close, the smooth relaxation of the discrete site count shrinks the site rather than closing it, so discrete transitions arrive late. Differentiable market clearing thus turns market-aware planning into a problem gradients can search.

</details>


### [312] [Unfolding the Leech Lattice: Fused Multi-Shell Decoding and VRAM Layouts for 2-Bit LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02652)
*Pier-Jean Malandrino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Leech-lattice矢量量化补上了文献中缺失的多壳解码器，并在batch=1的推理解码阶段测算了其GEMV服务成本。作者区分了VRAM内比特率与磁盘比特率，给出了四种比特级精确布局，在最紧配置下实现4.80 bits/weight（2.15x FP16）。部署的四位(AWQ)与二位(QTIP)GEMV内核表明，trellis内核比所测布局少读2.40x字节且快2.27x，时间差距与带宽差距吻合；同时揭示了有效包络（launch几何下限、第二内存层次上所有格分支均低于FP16）。端到端在4B、8B、14B上分别获1.11x、1.29x、1.41x加速，4B模型在2.60 GB内达87.0 tok/s。质量代价（4B时perplexity 1.38x、MMLU相差14.7分）随规模增大而缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leech-lattice矢量量化自报2-bit质量最强，但其内核仅解码一个壳层(shell)，而码率要求的多壳解码器在文献和实现中均缺失，导致该方法的实际解码阶段服务成本无人知晓。

Method: （1）构建完整301类码本的解码服务通路：离线展开为GPU布局，并设计无warp分化的融合"反量化+矩阵向量乘"内核，用f64精度验证。（2）将VRAM内比特率与磁盘比特率视为两个独立设计轴，在同一进程中比较四种比特级精确布局（二进制位平面胜于one-hot掩码）。（3）在同一进程中运行部署级四位(AWQ)与二位(QTIP)GEMV内核作为对照，并以无权重对照组及第二内存层次界定有效性包络。

Result: one-hot之下二进制位平面在恒定带宽下兼具更小体积与更快速度，达4.80 bits/weight（2.15x FP16）；低于4.3 bits需引入第二个不规则码流，3.6 bits时解码不再只是移位与掩码。对照表明trellis内核字节读取量仅为所供布局的1/2.40，速度快2.27x，时间差距与流量差距吻合。有效性包络：kaiser内核快于无权重对照，说明launch几何设定了下限；在第二内存层次，所有格分支均低于FP16。输出头一致时端到端在4B/8B/14B分别提升1.11x/1.29x/1.41x；采用int8输出头时4B模型在2.60 GB内达87.0 tok/s。质量方面4B时perplexity恶化1.38x、MMLU下降14.7分，随规模增大收窄。

Conclusion: 作者给出了首个可服务的Leech-lattice多壳解码GEMV路径，证明VRAM内布局是独立于磁盘码率的优化轴，并通过与部署级AWQ/QTIP内核的严格对照定位了本方案的性能定位——其瓶颈源于"展开一个对查找表而言过大的码本"的固有代价。整体上，小规模时以明显质量代价换取端到端延迟收益，质量差距随模型规模扩大而缩小。

Abstract: Leech-lattice vector quantization holds the strongest reported 2-bit quality under its own evaluation protocol. Its kernel decodes one shell; we found no implementation of the multi-shell decoder the rate requires. This paper supplies one and measures its serving cost for decode-phase GEMV at batch 1. First, a serving path for the full 301-class codebook: an offline expansion into GPU layouts and a fused dequantize-plus-matvec kernel reading them without warp divergence, verified against f64. Second, the in-VRAM rate is a design axis distinct from the on-disk rate. Four bit-exact layouts timed in one process show binary bit planes beating one-hot masks on size and speed at constant bandwidth (4.80 bits per weight, 2.15x FP16). Below 4.3 bits a second, irregular stream enters; at 3.6 the decode stops being shifts and masks. Third, deployed four-bit (AWQ) and two-bit (QTIP) GEMV kernels run in the same process. The trellis kernel reads 2.40x fewer bytes than our served layout and runs 2.27x faster at near-equal fractions of their byte bounds: the time gap tracks the traffic gap, the price of unfolding a codebook too large for a lookup table. Fourth, the validity envelope: the trellis kernel outruns our no-weights control, so our launch geometry sets that floor, and on a second memory hierarchy every lattice arm falls below FP16. With the output head held identical across arms, the kernel-and-format path gains 1.11x, 1.29x and 1.41x end to end at 4B, 8B and 14B; with an int8 output head the served 4B reaches 87.0 tok/s in 2.60 GB. The quality cost, 1.38x perplexity and 14.7 MMLU points at 4B, shrinks across the three sizes measured.

</details>


### [313] [H3DNAS: Hardware-Aware ONNX-Native 3D Point Cloud Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02684)
*Anchit Mulye,Rhythm Baghel,Sujay Kumar Ingle,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H3DNAS是一个无需源代码的硬件感知3D点云模型压缩框架，直接在ONNX计算图上进行通道剪枝与结构变异，在ModelNet40上将PointNet、PointNet++和PointMLP的参数分别减少65.5%、43.2%和49.1%，并取得1.99倍、1.29倍和1.67倍推理加速且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Jetson Orin Nano等边缘硬件上部署3D点云模型受限于计算和内存资源。现有压缩方法需要访问模型原始源代码，无法适用于厂商和模型仓库中常用的ONNX二进制格式。

Method: 三个核心贡献：(1) 通道依赖图(CDG)，将ONNX算子分为四类约束类，并在数学上证明自由参数比例ρ_f是拓扑不变量，可在O(|V|+|E|)时间内计算压缩上限；(2) 两阶段分层搜索：先通过L1重要性通道选择剪枝候选架构，再用零样本无标签输出保真度代理进行排序，最后对Pareto最优候选应用GhostConv结构变异；(3) 首个完全无需源码的3D点云模型压缩流水线，仅通过ONNX图手术实现。

Result: 在ModelNet40数据集上，PointNet、PointNet++和PointMLP参数分别减少65.5%、43.2%和49.1%，推理速度分别提升1.99倍、1.29倍和1.67倍，且精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: H3DNAS证明了在不访问原始源代码、无需梯度信息和架构类定义的前提下，仅通过对ONNX计算图进行硬件感知的通道剪枝和结构变异即可高效压缩3D点云模型，适用于边缘硬件部署场景。

Abstract: Deploying 3D point cloud models on edge hardware such as the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano is severely constrained by compute and memory budgets. Existing compression methods require access to the model's original source code, rendering them inapplicable to the Open Neural Network Exchange (ONNX) binaries commonly distributed by vendors and model repositories. We present \textbf{H3DNAS}, a hardware-aware model compression framework that operates directly on ONNX computational graphs without requiring original source code, architecture class definition, or gradient access during search. H3DNAS makes three contributions: (1) a \textbf{Channel Dependency Graph (CDG)} that classifies ONNX operators into four constraint classes and formally establishes that the free parameter fraction $ρ_f$ is topological invariant, a provable compression ceiling computable in $\mathcal{O}(|V|+|E|)$; (2) a \textbf{Two-Stage Hierarchical Search} that prunes candidate architectures by $L_1$-importance channel selection, ranks them by output fidelity as a zero-shot label-free proxy, and applies GhostConv structural mutation to Pareto-optimal candidates; and (3) the \textbf{first source-code-free compression pipeline for 3D point cloud models}, operating entirely via ONNX graph surgery with no original architecture definition required. On ModelNet40, H3DNAS reduces the number of parameters in PointNet, PointNet++, and PointMLP by $65.5\%$, $43.2\%$, and $49.1\%$, respectively, while achieving $1.99\times$, $1.29\times$, and $1.67\times$ inference speedups with negligible loss in accuracy. The source code is publicly available\footnote{https://github.com/ClarityLab-Org/h3dnas}.

</details>


### [314] [LoRA-TSD: Tangent-Space Spectral Descent for LoRA via Muon-Style Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02734)
*Dmitrii Andriianov,Andrey Veprikov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-TSD优化器，将LoRA更新视为固定秩矩阵流形上的切向量，在Muon的谱范数最速下降方向上更新，并通过流形上的retraction映射回LoRA因子。该方法比截断SVD回缩方案便宜高达2.8倍，首次给出LoRA-Pro和LoRA-TSD的全局收敛保证，在多个基准上优于现有LoRA优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调是标准做法，但其两个因子独立训练时忽略了低秩权重变化所诱导的几何结构。已有流形方法通常使用昂贵的截断SVD回缩操作，计算开销大，缺乏收敛性保证。

Method: 将每次LoRA步骤视为固定秩矩阵流形的切向量，在切空间内使用Muon的谱范数最速下降步骤，通过LoRA参数化原生兼容的retraction将结果映射回因子。推导Frobenius范数版本等价于LoRA-Pro，并证明切投影梯度（即Riemannian梯度）可作为自然平稳性度量，仅需因子梯度即可计算。

Result: 在6个常识和自然语言推理基准上（使用Llama-3.2-1B、Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen3-32B），LoRA-TSD优于所有对比的LoRA优化器，且对adapter秩保持鲁棒。retraction比截断SVD回缩方法便宜最高2.8倍。

Conclusion: LoRA-TSD是一种高效且具有理论保障的LoRA优化方法，通过引入流形几何视角同时提升了收敛性保证和实际性能，可用于大规模模型微调场景。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is the standard way to fine-tune large models, yet when its two factors are trained independently, the update ignores the geometry of the low-rank weight change it induces. We introduce LoRA-TSD, an optimizer that treats every LoRA step as a tangent vector of the fixed-rank matrix manifold and takes the spectral-norm steepest-descent step of Muon inside that tangent space, mapping the result back to the factors through a retraction native to the LoRA parametrization. The step avoids expensive operations on full weight matrices, and its retraction is up to $2.8\times$ cheaper than the truncated-SVD retraction used by prior manifold methods. We prove that the Frobenius-norm version of our surrogate recovers LoRA-Pro, and we identify the tangent-projected gradient, the Riemannian gradient of the manifold, as the stationarity measure natural to LoRA training and computable from the factor gradients alone. Under this measure we give the first global convergence guarantees for both LoRA-Pro and LoRA-TSD, with rates that drive the factor-gradient norms to zero. Across six commonsense and natural-language-inference benchmarks with Llama-3.2-1B, Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen3-32B, LoRA-TSD outperforms every competing LoRA optimizer and stays robust to the adapter rank. Code is available at https://github.com/brain-lab-research/LoRA-TSD.

</details>


### [315] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Know Physics? Contamination, Units, and the Deterministic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02766)
*Wassim Tenachi,Yashar Hezaveh,Laurence Perreault Levasseur,Pierre-Luc Bacon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究表格基础模型（TFMs）是否学到了物理学知识。通过用316个物理方程采样数据集评估4种TFM与6种基线，发现TFMs在域内外均表现优异，但其先验既不能表示无噪声的物理机制，也不能表示物理单位，因此能插值物理规律但尚不能作为物理模型使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格是大多数物理测量数据的格式，表格基础模型被训练为像语言模型填补文本一样填补表格。它们的贝叶斯性质决定了其预测质量取决于先验中蕴含的知识。核心问题在于：这类模型在学习表格填补的过程中，是否在内部隐含地捕获了物理规律？

Method: 从316个物理方程中采样数据集，对4种表格基础模型（TabPFN-3、TabICLv2、TabDPT和Real-TabPFN-2.5）和6种基线方法进行评估，分别在域内（in-domain）和域外（out-of-domain）条件下直接探测TFMs的先验内容。

Result: TFMs不论在开箱即用还是调参之后，在所有场景下均优于基线方法。然而，实验证明这些模型的先验既无法表示无噪声的物理机制，也无法编码物理单位的概念。这解释了为何它们能够在物理数据上实现有效的插值，却仍不能充当真正的物理学模型。

Conclusion: TFMs在物理表格数据的回归/填补任务上表现出显著的实用优势，但存在根本性局限：其先验缺乏对物理单位与确定性物理机制的建模能力。这意味着它们可以做物理规律的"逼近者"（interpolator），但还不是"物理模型"（physical model）。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) learn to fill in tables the way language models fill in text, and tables are arguably the format in which most physical measurement arrives. Did they learn any physics in the process? They are Bayesian by construction, so the question is what their prior contains. We probe it directly, evaluating four of them (TabPFN-3, TabICLv2, TabDPT and Real-TabPFN-2.5) against six baselines on datasets sampled from 316 physical equations, in and out of domain. TFMs dominate, out of the box and after tuning. But we show that their prior can represent neither a noiseless mechanism nor physical units, which is why they interpolate physics without yet being able to act as physical models.

</details>


### [316] [Cliff: Learning Process Rewards from the First Mistake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02817)
*Peixuan Han,Runhui Wang,Ketan Ramaneti,Jie Hao,Gerald Friedland,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cliff是一种奖励塑形策略，利用现成的LLM作为教师来识别每个rollout中的第一个错误，将推理过程自然地分解为正确前缀和错误后缀，并据此分配token级优势（正确前缀为正、错误后缀为负），从而为RLVR提供更细粒度的监督信号。在12个场景中，Cliff相比on-policy distillation提升15%，相比标准GRPO提升7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽已成为LLM后训练的有效范式，但其依赖粗粒度的结果奖励，对中间推理过程提供的指导有限。现有方法如过程奖励建模和on-policy distillation引入了额外约束（如依赖专门奖励模型、假设师生推理模式相同）。作者观察到一旦推理过程首次出错，后续推理的评估信息有限（因为已经基于无效前缀）。因此需要一个简单通用的方法，在不引入这些额外约束的情况下提供更丰富的监督。

Method: Cliff的核心思想是利用现成的off-the-shelf LLM作为教师，识别每个rollout中的第一个错误位置。这样rollout被自然地分解为两个部分：正确的前缀和不正确的后缀。随后，Cliff将此信号转换为token级别的优势值——对正确前缀分配正优势，对错误后缀分配负反馈（优势为负）。这是对标准GRPO的奖励塑形扩展，无需专门的奖励模型，也无需假设教师与学生具有相同的推理模式。

Result: 在12个不同场景的实验表明，Cliff持续提升推理性能，相比on-policy distillation提升15%，相比标准GRPO提升7%，即使在教师能力有限的情况下也能取得优势。此外，文章还分析了"ground truth"在Cliff中的作用，并研究了其训练动力学特征。

Conclusion: Cliff被验证为一种简单、通用且有效的RLVR改进方法，通过更丰富的细粒度监督信号来提升LLM的推理能力。其关键优势在于无需专门的过程奖励模型或教师-学生推理一致性假设，仅需现成LLM提供首次错误定位即可。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for large language model (LLM) post-training, but its reliance on coarse outcome rewards leads to limited guidance on intermediate reasoning processes. Existing approaches such as process reward modeling and on-policy distillation introduce additional constraints, such as reliance on a specialized reward model or assuming identical reasoning patterns between teacher and student. Nevertheless, we observe that once a reasoning process first goes wrong, evaluating the subsequent reasoning provides limited additional information, as it is already conditioned on an invalid prefix. Therefore, we propose Cliff, a reward shaping strategy that utilizes an off-the-shelf LLM as a teacher to identify the first mistake in each rollout. As a result, the rollout is naturally decomposed into two parts: a correct prefix and an incorrect suffix. Cliff then converts this signal into token-level advantages, assigning positive advantages for the correct prefix and negative feedback afterward. Experiments across 12 different scenarios demonstrate that Cliff consistently improves reasoning performance, outperforming on-policy distillation by 15% and standard GRPO by 7%, even with teachers of modest capability. Furthermore, we analyse the role of ``ground truth'' in Cliff and investigate its training dynamics. These results establish Cliff as a simple, general and effective approach for improving RLVR with richer, fine-grained supervision.

</details>


### [317] [UE5M3 FP4 Block Scaling for Stable Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02846)
*Robert Hu,Carlo Luschi,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种更简化的4位浮点（FP4）预训练配方，使用无符号E5M3块缩放替代NVIDIA Transformer Engine的随机Hadamard变换和BF16最终层豁免方案。基于该配方预训练的Nemotron-H 8B模型在近1900亿token上取得了更低的训练损失和验证损失，量化推理下游任务指标全面更高，且模型体token吞吐量提升21.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定的4位浮点（FP4）预训练很困难，因为E2M1有效载荷只能表示较窄的数值范围。NVIDIA Transformer Engine的解决方案通过当前张量缩放、随机Hadamard变换和BF16最终层来应对，但这些都增加了FP4矩阵乘法之外的工作量，配方复杂。

Method: 将E2M1有效载荷与无符号E5M3块缩放配对使用。E5M3更宽的范围允许周期性张量缩放。配方仅对反向梯度应用选择性随机舍入（selective stochastic rounding），省略了随机Hadamard变换，并在所有符合条件的内部线性层中使用FP4。

Result: 预训练了一个Nemotron-H 8B模型近190亿token。相比Transformer Engine，所提出的block-16配方最终窗口训练损失更低；在各自的量化推理策略下，验证损失（以保留集负对数似然衡量）更低；所有三个报告聚合上的量化推理下游点估计均更高。本机执行消融显示，同时移除RHT和BF16最终块豁免可将模型体token吞吐量提升21.2%。

Conclusion: 结果证明了使用更简化的配方进行端到端软件模拟FP4预训练的可行性，并为无符号E5M3块缩放的硬件原生支持提供了动机。

Abstract: Stable 4-bit floating-point (FP4) pretraining is difficult because the E2M1 payload represents only a narrow range of magnitudes. NVIDIA's Transformer Engine \nv{} recipe addresses this with current-tensor scaling, a randomized Hadamard transform (RHT), and bfloat16 (BF16) final layers, adding work outside the FP4 matrix multiplications. We instead pair E2M1 payloads with unsigned E5M3 (\ue{}) block scales. Their wider range permits periodic tensor scaling, while our recipe applies selective stochastic rounding to backward gradients, omits RHT, and uses FP4 in all eligible internal linears.
  We pretrain a Nemotron-H 8B model for nearly 190 billion tokens. Compared with Transformer Engine \nv{}, the proposed block-16 recipe finishes with lower final-window training loss and, under their respective quantized-inference policies, lower validation loss measured as held-out negative log-likelihood. Its quantized-inference downstream point estimates are also higher on all three reported aggregates. A native \nv{} execution ablation that jointly removes RHT and the BF16 final-block exemption increases measured model-body token throughput by 21.2\%. These results demonstrate end-to-end software-emulated \uefp{} pretraining with a simpler recipe and motivate native support for \ue{} block scaling.

</details>


### [318] [Post-Training Language Models for Gold-Medal Performance in Coding Competitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02849)
*Aleksander Ficek,Sean Narenthiran,Mehrzad Samadi,Somshubra Majumdar,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向国际编程竞赛的端到端大语言模型专业化流水线，结合大规模题目精选、合成推理轨迹、SFT和RL训练。通过该流水线训练的Nemotron系列模型在IOI 2025和IOI 2026上均显著超越人类金牌线，其中Ultra-CC系统在真实竞赛约束下以535.4/600的得分首次超过IOI问题集上的人类最高分（498.27）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际程序设计竞赛（如IOI、ICPC）代表了大语言模型在推理能力上面临的最具挑战性的场景。作者希望构建一个端到端的专业化训练方案，使AI不仅在离线评测中表现优异，更能在与人类选手相同的时间、网络和提交限制下首次超越顶尖人类选手。

Method: 提出了包含四个核心组件的端到端专业化流水线：(1) 大规模题目精选（22,000道）；(2) 合成推理轨迹生成；(3) 监督微调（SFT）；(4) 强化学习（RL）。共训练了两个模型：Nemotron-3-Nano-CC（30B-A3B，SFT+RL）和Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC（550B-A55B，仅SFT）。此外引入了GenCorrect测试时计算策略，通过迭代生成、评估和精炼多样化解题方案来提升推理质量。最终还针对IOI竞赛场景开发了专用的Ultra-CC系统，并在IOI 2026上进行前瞻性评估。

Result: 在IOI 2025上，Nano-CC从训练前的130分提升到后训练的291分，在GenCorrect加持下达到468分，超过438.3分的金牌阈值；Ultra-CC达到502分。基于这些结果开发的竞赛专用Ultra-CC系统在IOI 2026上与人类选手相同约束下取得535.4/600分，超过金牌线（361.12）和人类最高分（498.27）。这是首个在IOI问题集上超越最高分人类选手的AI系统。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了大语言模型通过大规模数据策展、SFT和RL等专业化训练手段，能够在最具挑战性的国际算法竞赛中超越顶尖人类选手，标志着AI在竞赛级推理能力上达到了里程碑式的进展。

Abstract: Competitive programming has become a key test of large language model reasoning, with international competitions such as IOI and ICPC representing its most challenging settings. We present an end-to-end specialization pipeline combining large-scale problem curation, synthetic reasoning traces, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL). Using 22,000 curated problems, we train Nemotron-3-Nano-CC (30B-A3B) with SFT and RL and Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC (550B-A55B) with SFT alone. We further introduce GenCorrect, a feedback-driven test-time compute strategy that iteratively generates, evaluates, and refines diverse solutions. On IOI 2025, Nano-CC improves from 130 points to 291 after post-training and to 468 with GenCorrect, exceeding the gold threshold of 438.3 while Ultra-CC reaches 502. Guided by these results, we develop a competition-specific Ultra-CC system and evaluate it prospectively during IOI 2026. Under the same time, internet-access, and submission constraints as human contestants, it scores 535.4 out of 600, exceeding both the gold threshold of 361.12 and the top human score of 498.27. To our knowledge, this is the first AI system to outscore the highest-scoring human contestant on an IOI problem set.

</details>


### [319] [The Implications of Linguistic Illegibility for LLM Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02852)
*James Mickens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"语言不可读性"（linguistic illegibility）概念，指LLM的外部语言输出或可机械化提取的语言特征无法准确反映其内部模型计算。由于LLM的内部计算本质上是激活空间上的数学运算，与自然语言之间存在有损翻译，因此依赖模型语言自我报告的安全机制（如链式思维监控、宪法自我批评、激活探针）永远无法完全可靠。作者主张基于污点跟踪（taint tracking）的沙箱隔离是更有前景的方向，并结合稳健虚拟化、第三方审计等机制为语言监控提供安全下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全机制大量依赖LLM的语言输出作为理解其内部推理的窗口，但有多种证据表明这些语言化产物并不可靠。作者旨在指出这一根本性缺陷，并论证为何需要转向不依赖语言状态读取的隔离技术。

Method: 通过理论论证阐述语言不可读性的不可避免性（内部计算为激活空间数学，与语言间存在有损翻译），并分析现有语言依赖型安全机制的缺陷；提出以污点跟踪为核心的沙箱方案，辅以稳健虚拟化和第三方审计等机制。

Result: 论证了语言不可读性对依赖语言自我报告的安全机制施加了根本性限制；提出污点跟踪可作为独立于模型语言状态的有效沙箱基础，并指出该方案及附加机制能够缓解前沿模型的沙箱逃逸问题。

Conclusion: 依赖模型语言自述的安全机制在原理上不可完全可靠，沙箱隔离技术（如污点跟踪、稳健虚拟化、第三方审计）应作为安全下限补充甚至替代语言监控，其保障不依赖于解读模型的任何语言状态。

Abstract: LLMs are trained to generate natural language. However, various strands of evidence indicate that an LLM's externalized linguistic outputs and mechanistically-extracted linguistic features can be an unreliable lens for understanding internal model computation. We introduce the term ``linguistic illegibility'' to broadly refer to scenarios in which an LLM's externalized or mechanistically-probed language artifacts fail to represent how the model actually thinks. We argue that the specter of linguistic illegibility is unavoidable for LLMs whose internal computations are not directly expressed via language, but rather math over activation spaces (with lossy translations between activation spaces and natural language happening at the bookends). If linguistic illegibility is always possible, then security mechanisms that rely on a model's linguistic self-reporting (e.g., chain-of-thought monitoring, constitutional self-critique, activation probing for linguistically-defined feature vectors) can never be completely sound; the model sandbox will always need isolation techniques whose guarantees do not depend on reading a model's linguistic state at all. We argue that observing a model's outputs using taint tracking is a promising approach for an effective sandbox: regardless of how a model linguistically self-reports, a taint tracking policy can define, a priori, various pieces of system state that should never be influenced by model-produced data. We also discuss several additional sandboxing mechanisms (e.g., robust virtualization, third-party auditing of sandboxing configurations) which collectively provide a critical floor beneath linguistic monitoring, and would have mitigated recent sandbox exploits by frontier models.

</details>


### [320] [Graph Machine: Towards Better Pretraining via Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02881)
*Lintai Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图机器（Graph Machine, GM）架构，通过可微指针追踪式路由机制维护O(n)大小状态，在稀疏动态路由下保持计算复杂度O(n)。用GM稀疏层替换Qwen3-0.6B中75%的Transformer层，在15.7B token上从头预训练，每KV头仅检索2/4096个token时损失轻微退化，检索4个时最佳模型损失略有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两难：固定大小状态的方法限制了可访问的状态规模，而稀疏但静态的路由虽然降低计算量却将可达状态限制在O(1)。作者希望在不将潜在可访问状态大小限制为O(1)的前提下，保持稀疏层的O(n)复杂度。

Method: GM使用O(n)大小的状态，通过边（edges）——一种指针样式的对象——以类似指针追逐（pointer chasing）的可微分发机制进行动态更新和路由。每条稀疏层中每个KV头仅检索部分token（如2或4个，来自总共4096个），同时维持O(n)计算复杂度。

Result: 在Qwen3-0.6B上替换75%的密集Transformer层为GM稀疏层，从头预训练15.7B tokens。每KV头只检索2个token时，损失仅轻微退化；检索4个token时，最佳模型在损失上略有改善。

Conclusion: GM架构证明了可以通过可微路由机制在保持O(n)稀疏计算复杂度的同时，维持O(n)可访问状态大小，且性能上与密集Transformer相当甚至更优，表明稀疏动态路由是一种可行的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce the Graph Machine (GM), an architecture that maintains an $O(n)$-sized state and accesses it through sparse, dynamic routing. Unlike methods with fixed-size states or sparse but static routing, GM preserves $O(n)$ complexity in its sparse layers without restricting the potentially accessible state size to $O(1)$. Instead, GM uses edges - pointer-like objects updated differentiably by a referral mechanism resembling pointer chasing. We replace 75% of the dense Transformer layers in Qwen3-0.6B with GM sparse layers and pretrain from scratch on 15.7B tokens. With only 2 of 4,096 tokens retrieved per KV head in each sparse layer, loss degrades only slightly; with 4, the best model marginally improves loss.

</details>


### [321] [A Common Measure of Communication for Speech Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02887)
*Dulhan Jayalath,Benjamin Ballyk,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出开放词汇互信息（OVMI），一种信息论度量，用于在统一尺度上评估不同语音脑机接口（speech BCI）系统的信息传达能力，解决该领域因数据集、记录方式和词表不同而导致的指标不可比较问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音BCI领域缺乏统一的进展度量标准：不同系统使用不同的数据集、记录方法、语音类型和词汇表，导致报告的性能分数难以横向比较。根本原因在于两个未解决的问题：(i) 语音BCI应支持用户沟通什么词汇分布；(ii) 系统能从该分布中传达多少信息。

Method: 作者推导了开放词汇互信息（OVMI）这一信息论度量，衡量解码器相对于用户可能希望沟通词汇的参考分布所传达的信息量，从而允许在不同条件下（如不同词汇表）测量的能力在共同的沟通尺度上评估。作者还展示了如何利用OVMI来优化词汇表选择。

Result: 研究表明，通常报告的准确率、词错误率（WER）及其他仅在系统支持的词汇上计算的指标会高估系统能传达的用户意图信息量。利用OVMI比较现有系统可揭示系统在语言覆盖范围与解码准确度之间的权衡；选择使OVMI最大化的词汇表在三个语音域上取得了高达16.3%的相对准确率提升。

Conclusion: OVMI为语音BCI社区提供了一种原则性的方法来比较异构系统、改进词汇表设计并衡量领域进展。

Abstract: Speech brain-computer interfaces (speech BCIs) translate neural activity into language, offering a path towards restoring speech for people with paralysis and, more broadly, enabling new forms of natural human-computer interaction. Despite this promise, the field lacks a common measure of progress because systems use different datasets, recording methods, types of speech, and vocabularies, so their reported scores are rarely comparable. Underlying this measurement problem are two unresolved questions: (i) what distribution of words should a speech BCI enable a user to communicate, and (ii) how much information from this distribution can a system convey. We address both by deriving open-vocabulary mutual information (OVMI), an information-theoretic quantity that measures the information conveyed by a decoder relative to a reference distribution over the words a user may wish to communicate. This allows capabilities measured under different conditions, such as distinct vocabularies, to be evaluated on a common communication scale. We show that ordinarily reported accuracy, word error rate (WER), and other metrics computed only over the words a system supports can overstate how much of a user's intended speech the system can communicate. We then use OVMI to compare existing systems, expose trade-offs between how much of the user's language a system supports and how accurately it decodes those words, show that these comparisons depend on what the user is expected to communicate, and demonstrate that selecting a vocabulary to maximise OVMI yields up to 16.3% relative improvement in accuracy across three speech domains. OVMI therefore provides the speech BCI community with a principled way to compare heterogeneous systems, improve vocabulary design, and measure progress in the field.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [322] [Not All Agreement Counts as Corroboration: Provenance-Conserving Multi-View Fusion for Typed Action Admission in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01662)
*Zekai Jin,Hanrong Zhang,Yihong Tang,Fei Hu,Zhen Dong,Yi Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PACT框架，将证据可数性（evidence countability）视为一种关系变量，用于实现保留来源的融合（provenance-conserving fusion）与类型化动作准入。在供给的来源划分（provenance partition）下，PACT保留单元内的坐标支撑，仅在单元间累加证据，并将未满足的释放条件映射为hold、confirm或fallback。实验表明，PACT能有效区分计算性多重性与证据性多重性，来源重新分配会使证据预算按预测移动，八倍相机内重复不改变响应，且无参考不一致的准入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于具身系统，预测一致性（predictive agreement）不足以判定证据是否支持行动，证据的来源属性同样关键。同一观测的重复推断会增加一致性但不增加证据；而来源局部（source-local）值无法揭示输出是否具有可分别计数的来源。现有方法忽视了证据的可数性这一关键维度。

Method: PACT将证据可数性建模为关系变量，基于供给的来源划分定义可计数单元。它保留每个单元内的坐标支撑（coordinatewise support），仅在单元间累加证据，并将未满足的释放条件映射为三种类型化动作：hold、confirm、fallback。方法论上证明了坐标meet是满足单例保真与插入不放大性的最大预算，并具有粗化单调性与固定划分稳定性。

Result: 在48个场景簇、31,200次评估中，PACT取得0.0861的ncsAURC。排除对抗共识臂后，来源划分聚合相对单例聚合将ncsAURC降低0.0557；在完整来源记录中，PACT优于原生分数，但共同后验峰分数下与嵌套Dirichlet差异缩小。在离线人机协作中，八倍相机内复制使每检查点720个类型化响应不变；相机分组PACT在60个情景中准入47/57个参考一致候选且无参考不一致准入。

Conclusion: PACT成功分离了计算性多重性与证据性多重性：一致性仅在来源划分允许分别累加时才构成确证。在给定假设下，来源局部值无法识别可数性，必须借助来源划分的关系信息进行证据预算管理，从而为具身系统的安全行动准入提供了更可靠的依据。

Abstract: For embodied systems, predictive agreement alone does not determine whether evidence warrants action; evidential origin matters. Repeated inference over one observation can multiply agreement without adding evidence, while source-local values do not reveal whether outputs have separately countable origins. PACT treats evidence countability as a relational variable for provenance-conserving fusion and typed action admission. A supplied provenance partition defines countable units. PACT retains coordinatewise support shared within each unit, accumulates only across units, and maps unmet release conditions to hold, confirm, or fallback. Under the stated assumptions, source-local values cannot identify countability; the coordinatewise meet is the greatest budget satisfying singleton fidelity and insertion non-amplification, with coarsening monotonicity and fixed-partition stability. Across 31,200 evaluations in 48 scene clusters, PACT attains a common-support normalized risk-coverage area (ncsAURC) of 0.0861. Excluding the constructed adversarial-consensus arm, provenance-partition aggregation reduces ncsAURC by 0.0557 relative to singleton aggregation, while the corroboration contrast vanishes. On complete-source records, native scores favor PACT, but a common posterior-peak score narrows its difference from nested Dirichlet and favors product fusion. Reassigning provenance over unchanged predictions moves evidence budgets as predicted. In offline human-robot collaboration, eightfold within-camera duplication leaves 720 typed responses per checkpoint unchanged; camera-grouped PACT admits 47 of 57 Qwen3-VL-32B reference-consistent candidates with no observed reference-inconsistent admission in 60 episodes. PACT separates computational from evidential multiplicity: agreement constitutes corroboration only when provenance permits separate accumulation.

</details>


### [323] [TriSAR: Task Coordination and Collision Avoidance for Aerial Robot Teams in Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01731)
*Aditya Anil Kapile,Pedro Machado,Isibor Kennedy Ihianle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在基于物理的Gazebo仿真中评估了五无人机协调系统TriSAR，采用2x2因子设计（GA与贪心分配策略 × 碰撞避免开启/关闭）对地震灾区应急救援场景进行验证。结果表明使能反应式斥力显著消除碰撞违规（统计显著），而GA分配的效能优势仅在斥力关闭时可检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机灾害响应系统需要任务分配与局部轨迹控制的协调配合，但在受控实验条件下，这两层协调对任务效率和操作安全的单独及组合贡献尚未得到充分表征。

Method: 采用2x2因子设计，比较两种任务分配策略（遗传算法与贪心适应度分配）在反应式碰撞避免启用或禁用时的表现。在统一场景（5架无人机、8个目标）中，对四种配置各进行30次随机性情节测试，基于Gazebo物理仿真评估，并使用Mann-Whitney检验与Welch's t检验进行统计分析。

Result: 在贪心分配下，启用斥力消除碰撞阈值违规（U=885, p=4.03x10^-12, r=0.97）；在GA分配下同样确认保护效果（U=675, p=1.26x10^-5, r=0.50）。GA分配在斥力启用时相对贪心无统计显著优势，但在斥力禁用时，在步数、路径长度和能量方面显著优于贪心分配（Welch's t检验，|g|介于0.92~1.76）。

Conclusion: 反应式斥力提供了显著且依赖于分配策略的安全效益；而GA任务分配的额外计算复杂度仅在斥力禁用时才带来可检测的任务效率优势。

Abstract: Multi-Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) disaster-response systems require coordinated task assignment and local trajectory control, yet the individual and combined contributions of these coordination layers to mission efficiency and operational safety remain insufficiently characterised under controlled experimental conditions. TriSAR is evaluated as a five-UAV coordination system operating in a physics-based Gazebo simulation of an earthquake-damaged urban environment. A 2 x 2 factorial design compares two task-allocation strategies (Genetic Algorithm and greedy fitness-based allocation) with reactive collision avoidance enabled or disabled. Each of the four configurations was evaluated over 30 stochastic episodes in a common scenario of five UAVs and eight targets. Under greedy allocation, enabling repulsion eliminated recorded collision-threshold violations, confirmed by a Mann-Whitney test (U = 885, p = 4.03 x 10^-12, rank-biserial r = 0.97). Under GA allocation, the same protective effect was confirmed (U = 675, p = 1.26 x 10^-5, rank-biserial r = 0.50). For mission-efficiency metrics, GA-based allocation showed no statistically detectable advantage over greedy allocation when repulsion was enabled, but a significant advantage in steps, path length, and energy when repulsion was disabled (Welch's t-tests, |g| between 0.92 and 1.76). These results show that reactive repulsion provides a substantial, allocation-dependent safety benefit, while the additional computational complexity of GA-based task allocation yields a detectable mission-efficiency benefit only when repulsion is disabled.

</details>


### [324] [Designing Versatile Samples for Learned Trajectory Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01799)
*Yaguang Li,Jiaru Zhang,Chuheng Wei,Can Cui,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对端到端驾驶策略中的轨迹评分器（scorer）训练数据不足的问题，设计了沿横向（朝向可行驶边界）和纵向（朝向领航车辆）两个轴扰动人类驾驶轨迹的生成器，构造更具信息量的正负样本，用于训练Transformer评分器。在冻结的DiffusionDrive和MeanFuser生成式规划器上分别达到90.1和90.4的EPDMS分数，较基线提升0.4和0.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶策略通过从候选轨迹池中选择一条轨迹来实现决策，评分器可以独立训练。然而，许多强规划器生成的候选轨迹集中在安全模态附近，导致评分器在决策边界附近缺乏监督信号，无法学到区分安全与不安全轨迹的精细能力。

Method: 设计了一种增强训练数据集的方法：（1）构建两个扰动生成器，分别沿横向（朝可驾驶边界）和纵向（朝前导车辆）扰动记录的人类驾驶轨迹；（2）由此生成比基础规划器候选池更具信息量的正负样本；（3）将基于Transformer的评分器附加到两个冻结的生成式规划器（DiffusionDrive和MeanFuser）上，并在NAVSIM navtrain数据集上训练。

Result: 在DiffusionDrive上达到90.1 EPDMS，在MeanFuser上达到90.4 EPDMS（使用ResNet-34），均由设计的训练数据集带来0.4和0.3 EPDMS的提升。

Conclusion: 通过对人类轨迹进行有意义的横向和纵向扰动来设计训练数据集，可以显著提升评分器在决策边界附近的判别能力，从而改善端到端驾驶策略的整体性能，且无需修改冻结的规划器和轨迹生成器。

Abstract: Many current end-to-end driving policies emit a pool of candidate trajectories and select one, which makes selection a separable component: a scorer can be retrained while the planner, its backbone, and its trajectory generator all stay frozen. However, many strong planners concentrate their proposals around safe mode, providing limited supervision near decision boundaries. In this work, we design a training dataset that provides more informative supervision for the scorer. In particular, we construct two generators that perturb the logged human trajectory along the two axes a vehicle can be displaced: laterally toward the drivable boundary and longitudinally toward a leading vehicle. The designed dataset produces more informative positive and negative samples than the base planner's proposal pool. We attach a transformer-based scorer to two frozen generative planners, DiffusionDrive and MeanFuser, and train it on the NAVSIM navtrain dataset. The results of the experiments show that we achieve 90.1 EPDMS on DiffusionDrive and 90.4 EPDMS on MeanFuser when using ResNet-34, with 0.4 and 0.3 EPDMS respectively, from the designed training dataset.

</details>


### [325] [One Demonstration, Many Objects: Generalizing Manipulation via Local Contact Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01938)
*Satvik Sharma,Samrat Sahoo,Huang Huang,Fei-Fei Li Jiajun Wu,Dorsa Sadigh,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DemoMimic是一种基于接触中心奖励的灵巧操作策略，通过引入精确接触奖励和局部几何运动模仿，实现了从仿真到真实世界的跨物体、跨手型迁移，在16个物体、4个任务、2种机器人手上的平均成功率达71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指机器人手的灵巧操作数据难以大规模采集；现有的从人类演示学习结合sim-to-real强化学习的方法存在两个问题：(i)缺少显式奖励来激励精确接触，导致真实世界性能弱；(ii)对未见过的物体实例泛化能力差。

Method: 提出DemoMimic策略，其核心思想是关注接触点附近的局部几何结构来操作物体。引入接触中心的奖励机制以激励精确接触并提升sim-to-real一致性，从而使单一真实世界策略能够跨形状、尺度、质量、摩擦力不同的物体迁移，只要局部接触结构保持一致。

Result: 真实世界消融实验显示，DemoMimic在16个物体、4个任务和2种机器人手型上实现了71%的成功率，且相比基线具有最小的sim-to-real性能下降。

Conclusion: DemoMimic通过接触中心奖励和局部接触几何建模有效提升了灵巧操纵策略的精确性、跨物体泛化能力及sim-to-real迁移鲁棒性，证明了该方法的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with multi-fingered robot hands promises human-level dexterity, but collecting large-scale dexterous robot hand data remains difficult. Learning from human demonstrations has emerged as a scalable alternative to robot teleoperation, providing strong priors on object interaction and contact strategies. Recent sim-to-real RL methods incorporate such priors, but often (i) omit rewards that explicitly incentivize precise contact, yielding weak real-world performance, and/or (ii) generalize poorly to unseen object instances. We propose DemoMimic (Dexterous Motion Mimic), a policy that manipulates objects by focusing on their geometry local to the contact points. Its contact-centric rewards encourage precise contact and improve sim-to-real consistency, yielding a single real-world policy that transfers across objects of varying shape, scale, mass, and friction wherever local contact structure is preserved. Real-world ablations show that DemoMimic achieves 71% success across 16 objects, four tasks, and two robot-hand embodiments, with the smallest sim-to-real drop compared to baselines.

</details>


### [326] [MACAW: Reliable And Efficient Surgical Debridement Using Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01961)
*Ziyang Chen,Shutong Jin,Preethi Satish,Sareena Mann,Cael Magner,Danyal Fer,Omid Mohareri,Gary Guthart,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于手术清创的增强灵巧系统，通过视觉伺服实现相机平面内对准，并提出MACAW（单目自适应紧凑注意力窗口）方法实现深度控制。在dVRK机器人上进行了100次物理实验，单臂模式下成功率93%，吞吐量304块/小时；双臂模式下成功率92%，吞吐量提升至473块/小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强外科医生的灵巧度可使其从繁琐的细分子任务中解放出来。手术清创（移除病变或坏死组织碎片）因空间感知不精确和线缆驱动带来的不确定性而极具挑战性，亟需开发自动化系统以提升手术效率与精度。

Method: 提出增强灵巧系统，采用两部分策略：(1) 视觉伺服用于将线缆驱动夹爪与图像平面中的目标位置对齐；(2) 提出MACAW（Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows）新型深度控制方法，利用单目视觉构建自适应紧凑注意力窗口进行深度估计与操控。系统在da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK)上进行验证，并扩展至双臂清创配置。

Result: 在100次物理实验中，相机帧伺服在平均0.39秒内、4次优化步骤内将夹爪平均位置偏移从37像素降至5像素以内。MACAW显著优于程序化基线和学习型VLA基线，单臂模式成功率达93%，平均每个组织块11秒，吞吐量304块/小时；双臂模式保持92%成功率，平均每个组织块7秒，吞吐量提升至473块/小时。

Conclusion: MACAW方法在手术清创任务中展现出显著优势，不仅在单臂模式下取得高成功率与高吞吐量，扩展至双臂设置后进一步提升了效率，证明了视觉伺服结合单目自适应注意力窗口的深度控制方案在自主手术子任务中的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Augmenting the dexterity of human surgeons has the potential to free them from tedious subtasks. We consider debridement (removal of diseased or dead tissue fragments), which is challenging due to imprecision in spatial perception and cable actuation. We develop an augmented dexterity system for surgical debridement that uses visual servoing to align the cable-driven gripper with the target position in the image plane, and then introduces a novel approach to depth control, MACAW: Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows. Across 100 physical trials using the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) robot, camera-frame servoing reduced average gripper position offset from 37 to fewer than 5 pixels within 4 optimization steps, taking an average of only 0.39s. MACAW significantly outperforms procedural and learned VLA baselines, achieving a 93% success rate at 11 seconds per fragment, yielding a throughput of 304 fragments per hour. Extending MACAW to a bimanual debridement setup maintains a 92% success rate at an average of 7 seconds per fragment, increasing the throughput to 473 fragments per hour.

</details>


### [327] [Design and Validation of a Lightweight, Low-Profile Powered Knee Prosthesis with Quasi-Direct Drive Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02003)
*Ross J. Cortino,Ryan Posh,Emily G. Keller,Robert D. Gregg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款新型轻量化（2.6 kg）、低剖面（24.5 cm）的准直驱（QDD）膝关节假肢的设计与验证。通过优化两级传动机构及热/结构有限元分析，该设备实现了145 Nm的峰值扭矩，同时具备低反向驱动扭矩（1 Nm）和精确的位置/扭矩控制能力。三名膝上截肢者在平地行走和坐站转换测试中展示了仿生运动学和接近健全个体的峰值膝关节伸展扭矩，确立了QDD假肢的临床可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统被动膝关节假肢无法执行受控正功，迫使使用者在耗能活动中采取代偿行为。虽然QDD执行器在扭矩控制、反向驱动性和声学噪声方面优于传统高减速比执行器，但此前QDD原型机过于笨重，难以实现商业转化，因此需要开发轻量化、紧凑型QDD动力膝关节。

Method: 采用18:1两级传动系统优化，结合热学和结构有限元分析来降低设备质量；通过台架测试验证高输出扭矩、低反向驱动扭矩（1 Nm）及精确的位置与扭矩控制能力；并组织三名不同K级别（功能分级）的膝上截肢者进行平地行走和坐站转换实验验证。

Result: 设备总质量仅2.6 kg，构建高度24.5 cm，峰值扭矩达145 Nm，反向驱动扭矩低至1 Nm。三项人体实验展示了仿生运动学特征，峰值膝关节伸展扭矩处于健全个体参考值的一个标准差范围内，并在质量、构建高度、峰值扭矩和声学噪声方面达到或优于领先商业动力膝关节水平。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了QDD动力膝关节假肢在质量、尺寸和性能方面已具备临床可行性，有望取代传统高减速比动力假肢，为用户提供更优的动态性能。

Abstract: Fully-powered knee prostheses, unlike traditional passive knees, can perform controlled positive work, reducing the need for compensatory behaviors by users during energy-intensive activities. While quasi-direct drive (QDD) actuators provide superior torque control, backdrivability, and acoustic noise properties compared to traditional highly-geared actuators, prior QDD prototypes have been too heavy and bulky for commercial translation. In this work, we present the design and validation of a new lightweight (2.6 kg) and low-profile (24.5 cm tip-to-tip build height) QDD knee prosthesis. By optimizing an 18 to 1 two-stage transmission alongside thermal and structural finite-element analyses, we significantly reduce device mass while enabling a peak torque of 145 Nm. Through benchtop tests, we validate the device's high output torque, low backdrive torque (1 Nm), and its precision position and torque control capabilities. We also demonstrate biomimetic kinematics and peak knee extension torques (within one standard deviation of able-bodied references) during both level-ground walking and sit-stand transitions performed by three participants with transfemoral amputation and varying K-levels. By meeting or improving upon the mass, build height, peak torque, and acoustic noise of a leading commercial powered knee, this work establishes the clinical viability of emerging QDD prostheses that promise improved dynamic performance for their users.

</details>


### [328] [Real-Time Dynamics-Based Torque-Sampling MPPI for Compliant and Force Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02020)
*Euncheol Im,Taehyun Kim,Yonghwan Oh,Myotaeg Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型预测路径积分（MPPI）的任务空间控制框架，用于机器人操作器的安全物理交互。该框架在实时MPC公式中显式求解刚体动力学并强制执行安全约束，通过扭矩采样架构利用GPU并行化，实现了166 Hz以上的求解器更新率和0.18 s预测时域，并在7自由度机械臂上完成了真实世界实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的MPC方法在处理非线性刚体动力学时难以实时求解，无法满足机器人在非结构化环境中进行安全、柔顺物理交互的需求。需要一种能够高效处理非线性动力学、同时保证实时性和安全约束的控制框架。

Method: 提出基于MPPI的任务空间控制框架，在实时MPC公式中显式求解刚体动力学并强制执行安全约束；采用基于扭矩采样的控制架构，充分利用GPU并行化加速，实现精确的运动和力控制以产生柔顺行为。

Result: 所提框架实现了超过166 Hz的求解器更新率（预测时域0.18 s），并已在7自由度机械臂上通过真实世界实验验证了其性能，展示了有效的柔顺和力感知行为。

Conclusion: 该MPPI-based任务空间控制框架能够高效处理非线性动力学并满足实时约束，为机器人在非结构化环境中实现安全有效的物理交互提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This study proposes a novel Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI)-based task-space control framework. The proposed framework explicitly solves rigid-body dynamics within a real-time MPC formulation and enforces safety constraints, enabling accurate motion and force control that yields compliant behaviors for safe and effective physical interaction of robotic manipulators in unstructured environments. By leveraging MPPI, the proposed framework efficiently handles nonlinear dynamics that are difficult to solve with conventional MPC approaches in real-time. Furthermore, we develop a torque-sampling-based control architecture that enables efficient exploitation of GPU-based parallelization, resulting in effective compliant and force-aware behaviors. As a result, the proposed framework achieves a solver update rate of over 166 Hz with a 0.18 s prediction horizon, and its performance is validated through real-world experiments on a 7-DoF manipulator.

</details>


### [329] [Modeling What Changes: Sparse, Residual World Models for Object-Centric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02046)
*Param Thakkar,Parsika Paresh Shah,Manisha Sushant Gote*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种稀疏/残差世界模型，通过显式建模"变化"（每物体的变化门控+残差增量头）而非整体预测下一状态，在桌面上推物体任务中大幅提升预测精度（2.5-4.6倍）、大幅减少参数量（8.6-11.1倍），并具备零重训练的跨物体数量迁移能力，且在规划任务中展现出潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整体式（monolithic）世界模型每一步都预测整个下一状态，浪费容量在重复预测场景中静态不变的大多数内容上，并在此过程中向预测注入误差。作者质疑：显式建模"变化"（仅扰动被门控标记的物体）是否是物理预测与控制中更有效且更具可解释性的偏差。

Method: 提出稀疏/残差世界模型架构，包含两个核心组件：1）每物体变化门控（per-object change gate），用于判断哪些物体发生变化；2）残差增量头（residual delta head），仅对被门控标记的物体施加扰动预测。在MuJoCo桌面上推基准上，从3到8个物体进行缩放实验，并与密集多层感知机基线对比。

Result: 在MuJoCo桌面上推任务中（3到8个物体）：下一状态姿态预测准确度提升2.5-4.6倍，参数量减少8.6-11.1倍；变化检测F1达0.80-0.87（密集基线退化）；跨物体数零重训练迁移，F1保留99.4%；仅用四分之一数据达到约90%全数据精度；自回归推演中误差累积显著更少；在采样规划器下稀疏模型开始成功规划（0.23±0.06成功率），而密集整体模型在所有种子下均为零。

Conclusion: 建模"什么在变化"比重新预测整个世界更简单有效，是对物体中心物理AI的一种有价值的偏差归纳。该架构在精度、参数效率、泛化能力和规划可用性方面全面超越整体式密集基线。

Abstract: Monolithic world models predict the entire next state at every step, spending capacity re-predicting the static majority of a scene and injecting error into it. We ask whether explicitly modeling change (a per-object change gate plus a residual delta head that perturbs only the objects the gate flags) is a more effective and interpretable bias for physical prediction and control. On a MuJoCo tabletop pushing benchmark scaling from 3 to 8 objects, the sparse/residual model predicts next-state poses 2.5 to 4.6 times more accurately than a dense multilayer perceptron at 8.6 to 11.1 times fewer parameters, sustains change-detection F1 of 0.80 to 0.87 where the dense baseline is degenerate, transfers across object counts with zero retraining (99.4 percent F1 retention), and reaches about 90 percent of its full-data accuracy with a quarter of the data. In autoregressive rollout it compounds far less error, hugging the no-motion floor while the dense model drifts. Finally, inside a sampling-based planner, prediction-only models fail (though a true-simulator oracle solves the task with the identical planner, confirming the planner is sound), but once featurized and trained for the states a planner visits, the sparse model begins to plan (0.23 plus or minus 0.06 success over three seeds) while the dense monolith stays at zero at every seed. Modeling what changes, rather than re-predicting the whole world, is a simple, effective bias for object-centric physical AI; code, data generators, and all checkpoints will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [330] [Koopman-Based Robust Model Predictive Control for Nonlinear Systems with Stochastic Intermittent Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02079)
*Guanhua Liu,Tong Wu,Lixian Zhang,Weifeng Du,Minghao Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Koopman的随机模型预测控制（MPC）框架，用于处理间歇性状态测量下受约束非线性系统的控制问题。通过Lipschitz约束的深度Koopman模型提供线性潜在预测器，采用两模式马尔可夫链建模间歇测量过程，并结合概率截断软约束实现递归可行性和均方最终有界性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间歇性状态测量对约束非线性系统的MPC构成根本性挑战：反馈中断期间预测不确定性增长，测量触发重置会破坏标称状态传播，可能损害闭环稳定性和递归可行性。现有方法难以在间歇测量场景下同时保证计算效率和理论性质。

Method: 1) 采用Lipschitz约束的深度Koopman模型获取线性潜在预测器，实现高效在线优化；2) 将间歇测量过程建模为两模式离散时间马尔可夫链，形成统一的马尔可夫跳变误差模型，统一处理开环传播和测量触发重置；3) 推导数值可验证的充分条件，证明预测误差均方最终有界并获得显式一致二阶矩界；4) 构造无分布的概率误差半径（给定置信水平），用于截断依赖丢弃率的约束收紧；5) 采用精确惩罚软约束机制应对重置引起的跳变和长时间数据丢失。

Result: 在终端兼容性和有界扰动条件下，建立了递归可行性和闭环调节误差的均方最终有界性。数值仿真在一个视觉伺服跟踪任务上验证了理论结果，展示了在随机测量不可用情况下的有效跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为间歇性测量下的非线性约束MPC提供了一种鲁棒且计算高效的解决方案，通过Koopman线性化、马尔可夫误差建模及概率软约束收紧的结合，实现了理论可验证的稳定性和可行性保证。

Abstract: Intermittent state measurements pose fundamental challenges to model predictive control of constrained nonlinear systems because prediction uncertainty grows during feedback outages and measurement-triggered resets disrupt nominal state propagation, potentially compromising closed-loop stability and recursive feasibility. This paper develops a Koopman-based stochastic MPC framework with probabilistically truncated soft constraints. Specifically, a Lipschitz-constrained deep Koopman model provides a linear latent predictor, enabling computationally efficient online optimization. The intermittent measurement process is modeled as a two-mode discrete-time Markov chain, yielding a unified Markov jump error model for open-loop propagation and measurement-triggered resets. Under numerically verifiable sufficient conditions, the prediction error is shown to be mean-square ultimately bounded, and an explicit uniform second-moment bound is obtained. A distribution-free probabilistic error radius is then constructed for a prescribed confidence level and used to truncate dropout-dependent constraint tightening. An exact-penalty soft-constraint mechanism accommodates reset-induced jumps and prolonged dropouts. Under the stated terminal compatibility and bounded-disturbance conditions, recursive feasibility and mean-square ultimate boundedness of the closed-loop regulation error are established. Numerical simulations on a visual-servoing tracking task corroborate these theoretical results and demonstrate effective tracking under stochastic measurement unavailability.

</details>


### [331] [Unified Motion Retargeting for Humanoids with Learned Point Cloud Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02134)
*Hanyang Cao,Yuetong Fang,Taesoo Kwon,Runyi Yu,Ji Ma,Jing Tan,Yangchen Zhou,Baoze Du,Yi Gu,Yukang Gao,Ruoli Dai,Lei Han,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Unified Motion Retargeting (UMR)框架，通过学习稠密点云对应关系而非手动设计的人-机器人关键点映射，将人类运动数据重定向到人形机器人，实现跨异构运动源、机器人形态和下游场景的统一运动重定向，且优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的运动学习依赖将大规模人类运动数据转化为机器人参考轨迹，但人与机器人在形态、自由度、关节范围等方面存在显著差异。现有重定向方法依赖手工设计的稀疏关键点或身体部位对应，质量高度依赖人工语义设计，难以跨运动源和机器人形态扩展，且对精细姿态和交互的指导不足。

Method: UMR以外部点云作为人与机器人之间的统一接口，通过学习稠密点云对应关系解耦重定向过程与源骨骼语义和机器人拓扑结构。具体包括：(1) 学习无需手动人-机器人映射的稠密点云对应；(2) 利用稠密对应提供细粒度几何锚点，进行带约束的点云匹配优化；(3) 实现表面级姿态对齐和交互接触的直接迁移。

Result: 实验表明UMR统一了异构运动源、不同机器人形态及从运动到交互等多种下游场景的重定向，在运动保真度和合理性方面均优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: UMR为将大规模人类运动参考数据转化为机器人就绪训练数据提供了可扩展的基础框架。

Abstract: Humanoid learning increasingly relies on transforming vast and diverse human motion data into high-quality robot reference trajectories. However, retargeting human motion to humanoid robots is challenging due to substantial differences in morphology, degrees of freedom, joint ranges, and kinematic constraints between humans and robots. Existing retargeting methods typically address these differences by defining human-robot correspondence through hand-crafted sparse keypoints or body-part pairs. As a result, retargeting quality depends heavily on manual semantic design, limiting scalability across motion sources and robot morphologies and providing only sparse guidance for reproducing detailed poses and interactions. In this paper, we present Unified Motion Retargeting (UMR), a framework that learns dense point cloud correspondence without requiring manually designed human-robot mappings. By treating exterior point clouds as a unified interface between human motion and humanoid robots, UMR decouples retargeting from source-specific skeletal semantics and robot-specific topology. The learned dense correspondence provides fine-grained geometric anchors for constrained point cloud matching optimization, enabling surface-level pose alignment and direct transfer of interaction contacts. Experiments demonstrate that UMR unifies retargeting across heterogeneous motion sources, robot embodiments, and downstream scenarios ranging from locomotion to interaction, while achieving higher motion fidelity and plausibility than state-of-the-art methods. UMR therefore provides a scalable foundation for transforming large-scale human motion references into robot-ready training data.

</details>


### [332] [Towards Effective Physical Reservoir Computing with a Pneumatic Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02157)
*Jeevan Hebbal Manjunath,Jun Wang,Suyi Li,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用带有五气囊传感柱的气动软臂，系统研究了物理储备池计算（PRC）中气囊互连拓扑、机器人刚度和传感数量对弯曲角度估计性能的影响。通过36组匹配实验发现三条设计准则：独立密封气囊保留更丰富的可观测状态；增大传感柱基线压力会使气囊响应冗余化并显著增加耦合拓扑的估计误差；在密封拓扑下，两个传感器即可获得大部分性能收益，三个传感器几乎达到全部性能，更多传感器几乎无额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储备池计算（PRC）利用物理动力学系统作为计算资源进行状态估计和控制，但缺乏关于如何更有效设计物理储备池的正式设计规则研究。

Method: 使用带五气囊传感柱的气动软臂，在36组匹配试验中（涵盖波形、传感柱基线压力、驱动范围等条件），通过改变气囊互连拓扑、机器人刚度和仪器化传感器数量，以0.2秒压力历史和固定岭估计器在相同时间弯曲角度估计基准下评估所有设计。

Result: 得出三条设计指南：(1) 独立密封气囊比共享流形保留更丰富的可观测状态；(2) 增加传感柱基线压力使气囊响应更冗余，在耦合拓扑中显著增大估计误差；(3) 密封拓扑中两个策略性布置的传感器即可恢复大部分可达收益，三个传感器捕获几乎全部收益，额外传感器几乎不增加价值。

Conclusion: 拓扑、刚度和传感器数量应协同设计以实现软机器人状态的高精度PRC；仅靠更强的激励无法恢复因不良设计选择而丢失的多样性。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing (PRC) refers to the use of a physical dynamical system as a computational resource for tasks such as state estimation and control, but there has been a lack of formal study of design rules towards more effective design of such physical reservoirs. Using a pneumatic soft arm with a five-pouch sensing column, this work studies how the pouch interconnection topology, robot stiffness, and the number of instrumented sensors affect bending-angle estimation performance. Across 36 matched trials spanning waveform, baseline pressure of the sensing column, and actuation range, all designs are evaluated under the same-time bending-angle estimation benchmark using 0.2 s of pressure history and a fixed ridge estimator. Our analysis of the experimental results leads to three design guidelines. First, independently sealed pouches preserve a much richer observable state than a shared manifold. Second, increasing the baseline pressure of the sensing column makes the pouch responses more redundant and increases estimation error most strongly in the coupled topology. Third, in the sealed topology, two strategically placed sensors already recover most of the attainable benefit, three capture essentially all of it, and additional sensors provide little or no additional value.
