In summary, the results suggest that topology, stiffness, and number of instrumented sensors should be co-designed for accurate PRC of soft robot states; stronger excitation alone cannot recover the diversity that poor design choices have already removed.

</details>


### [333] [Hardware-Accelerated Instance Segmentation for Resource-Constrained Space Robotics with Criticality Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02219)
*Siddhant Shete,Hilmi Dogu Kücüker,Udo Frese,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向资源受限月球机器人的部署级实例分割框架，联合解决量化校准、辐射故障暴露与计算约束三大难题，在微型月球车上实现309ms推理延迟和5.7W功耗，恢复69.8%的量化精度损失并将全局关键度降低31.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主月球任务要求在极低光照、有限机载算力和辐射诱导硬件故障三重耦合约束下进行实时感知。现有方法未能同时应对量化校准与系统级故障暴露问题，缺乏在严格资源约束条件下可靠部署AI感知框架的综合方案。

Method: 提出AVIS（激活方差信息采样）标签无关校准策略，基于激活方差统计确定性筛选校准样本；在深度学习处理器单元上部署基于YOLO的分割模型，通过架构改进减少CPU回退路径并实现低光照下的静态编译执行和有界延迟；引入软件级关键性分析以估计故障暴露并指导辐射约束操作下的缓解措施。

Result: 在月球微型漫游车平台上，AVIS结合偏置校正恢复了69.8%的量化诱导精度损失，实现309ms推理延迟和5.7W功耗；定向缓解将全局关键性降低31.7%。

Conclusion: 结果证明了在太空部署约束下，该集成方法可作为可靠且安全的AI感知框架蓝图，为资源受限环境下联合处理量化与辐射故障问题提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous lunar missions require real-time per- ception under three coupled constraints: extreme low-light conditions, limited onboard compute, and radiation-induced hardware faults that can silently corrupt inference. We present a deployment-oriented instance segmentation framework for resource-constrained lunar robotics that jointly addresses quan- tization calibration and system-level fault exposure under strict compute constraints. First, we introduce Activation Variance Informative Sampling (AVIS), a label-free calibration strategy that deterministically selects calibration samples based on activation variance statistics. Second, we deploy a YOLO-based segmentation model on a Deep Learning Processor Unit (DPU) with architectural modifications that reduce CPU fallback paths and enable statically compiled execution with bounded latency in low-lighting conditions. We further introduce a software-level criticality analysis to estimate fault exposure and guide mitigation under radiation-constrained operation. On a lunar micro-rover platform, AVIS with bias correction recovers 69.8% of quantization-induced accuracy loss while achieving 309 ms inference latency and 5.7 W power consumption. Targeted mitigation reduces global criticality by 31.7%. The results demonstrate an integrated approach and a blueprint for a reliable and safe AI perception framework under space deployment constraints.

</details>


### [334] [FOCUS: Foot Observation Confidence for Robust Humanoid Proprioceptive Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02222)
*Kaixin Feng,Angsong Li,Shaopeng Zhang,Enyu Li,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,You Li,Haiyu Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FOCUS方法，用连续的正向运动学（FK）可靠性权重取代二值接触判断，以提升带腿机器人本体感知里程计的估计精度。网络通过无标注模拟数据训练，仅依赖IMU和关节运动学测量，将预测权重用于融合FK速度与IMU传播速度并自适应EKF观测协方差，在模拟和真实实验中显著降低了绝对轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的接触辅助里程计方法依赖二值接触判断决定是否信任整只脚的FK测量，但动态运动场景中常出现部分支撑、脚趾拖地和脚滑等情况，二值接触决策无法反映FK的实际可靠性，会导致长轨迹上累积显著漂移。

Method: FOCUS通过预测每个脚的连续FK可靠性权重而非二值接触状态；权重用于将FK速度观测与IMU传播的机体速度进行平滑混合，并自适应调整扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）的观测协方差，实现无硬接触切换的可靠性感知融合。网络使用自动生成的模拟信号，通过FK加权速度一致性损失和轻量模拟器接触正则化训练，无需人工标注连续FK可靠性标签。部署时仅需IMU与关节运动学测量。

Result: 在仿真行走场景中ATE降低83.7%；保持仿真动态运动的尺度与频谱能量保真度；在19段真实行走数据上ATE降低70.8%；在四套真实动态运动方案上平均ATE降低42.7%。

Conclusion: FOCUS通过以连续可靠性权重取代二值接触，有效解决了动态运动中的FK不可靠问题，显著提升了带腿机器人本体感知里程计的精度，且在实际硬件上部署无需依赖不可靠的扭矩传感。

Abstract: Foot forward kinematics (FK) is widely used to improve proprioceptive legged odometry by providing reliable velocity constraints during foot support. Existing contact-aided estimators generally rely on binary contact decisions to determine whether the FK measurements of an entire foot should be trusted. However, contact does not necessarily imply FK reliability. Dynamic locomotion often involves partial support, toe dragging, and foot slip, causing binary contact decisions to accumulate significant drift over long trajectories. To address this limitation, we propose FOCUS (Foot Observation Confidence from Unannotated Simulation), which predicts a continuous FK reliability weight for each foot instead of estimating binary foot contact. Rather than replacing the model-based estimator, the predicted reliability weights are used to blend FK velocity observations with IMU-propagated body velocity and to adapt the observation covariance of an extended Kalman filter (EKF), enabling smooth reliability-aware fusion without hard contact switching. The network is trained from automatically generated simulation signals using an FK-weighted velocity consistency loss with lightweight simulator-contact regularization, without manually annotated continuous FK-reliability labels. The deployed model relies only on IMU and joint kinematic measurements, making it suitable for hardware platforms with unreliable torque sensing. Experiments demonstrate that FOCUS reduces absolute trajectory error (ATE) by 83.7% on simulated walking episodes, preserves simulated dynamic-motion fidelity in motion scale and spectral energy, reduces ATE by 70.8% across 19 real walking segments, and reduces mean ATE by 42.7% across four real dynamic-motion routines.

</details>


### [335] [DiffuSearch: How Hybrid Trajectory Planning Benefits from Aligned Objectives in Diffusion and Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02252)
*Steffen Hagedorn,Aron Distelzweig,Alexandru P. Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffuSearch是一种新型混合规划器，通过统一的目标函数（碰撞避免、可行驶区域合规、舒适性和进展）贯穿轨迹生成与精化两个阶段。它采用两步架构：首先用引导扩散模型生成场景一致的联合轨迹预测，将驾驶目标作为可微引导函数；其次用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）在离散动作空间中进行显式局部精化，使用相同的驾驶目标作为奖励函数。在nuPlan和interPlan基准上取得了强或最先进性能，显著减少碰撞并提升舒适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶混合规划架构通常由多个目标各异、彼此分离的模块组成，缺乏统一原则，导致初始轨迹与精化轨迹之间不一致，进而产生次优行为。

Method: DiffuSearch采用两阶段架构：第一阶段使用引导扩散模型生成场景一致、联合轨迹预测，驾驶目标作为可微引导函数隐式引导去噪过程；第二阶段在离散动作空间中执行蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），对扩散模型生成的提议进行显式局部精化，并使用相同的驾驶目标作为奖励函数，实现隐式引导与显式搜索的目标统一。

Result: 在nuPlan和interPlan反应式闭环基准上，DiffuSearch取得了强或最先进的性能，尤其在复杂交互场景中大幅减少碰撞并提升舒适性。消融实验表明MCTS精化是性能提升的主要机制，而共享隐式引导与显式搜索目标可带来进一步的一致性改进。

Conclusion: 通过共享统一的驾驶目标集，扩散模型（擅长场景一致解探索）与MCTS（可解释、约束感知的局部精化）能够协同工作，为混合规划架构提供了一种统一原则，有效解决模块间不一致问题并提升规划质量。

Abstract: In trajectory planning for autonomous driving, hybrid planning architectures are often realized as a collection of disparate modules, each with its own objectives. This lack of a unifying principle can lead to inconsistencies between the initial and refined trajectory, resulting in suboptimal behavior. We address this by introducing DiffuSearch, a novel hybrid planner that uses a unified set of objectives across generation and refinement. Our model encourages all components to follow the same shared driving goals: collision avoidance, drivable area compliance, comfort, and progress. DiffuSearch employs a two-stage architecture. First, a guided diffusion model generates a scene-consistent, joint trajectory prediction, using our driving objectives as differentiable guidance functions to implicitly steer the denoising process. Second, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) in a discretized action space performs an explicit, local refinement of this proposal, leveraging the same driving objectives as its reward function. This synergistic design leverages the diffusion model's strength in finding scene-consistent solutions combined with the explainable, constraint-aware refinement of MCTS. Experiments on nuPlan and interPlan reactive closed-loop benchmarks demonstrate that DiffuSearch achieves strong and often state-of-the-art performance, substantially reducing collisions and improving comfort, particularly in complex, interactive scenarios. Our ablation studies indicate that MCTS refinement is the main mechanism behind the gains, while sharing objectives between implicit guidance and explicit search provides further consistent improvements.

</details>


### [336] [CrashDiffuser: VLM-Guided Collision Intent Reasoning for Fine-Grained Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02270)
*Shucheng Zhang,Yuang Zhang,Bingzhang Wang,Muhammad Monjurul Karim,Kehua Chen,Yinhai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CrashDiffuser，一种基于VLM引导的闭环扩散框架，用于细粒度安全关键场景生成——不仅要求目标碰撞，还要求碰撞发生在指定的车头、车尾或侧面接触区域。在WOMD闭环场景中，单次尝试目标碰撞率达50.33%，三次尝试达67.98%，接触区域控制成功率为40.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全关键场景生成器主要聚焦于诱导碰撞，缺乏对目标车辆碰撞接触位置的细粒度控制能力。而实际自动驾驶评测中，不同碰撞位置（车头、车尾、侧面）对系统的感知和决策能力构成完全不同的挑战，因此需要能够精确控制碰撞接触区域的场景生成方法。

Method: CrashDiffuser采用闭环VLM引导的扩散框架。具体包括：(1) 通过层次化碰撞意图接口，将语义级别的碰撞推理与连续轨迹合成解耦；(2) 初始化阶段，VLM提取可复用的场景级上下文；(3) 每次重规划步骤中，VLM预测包含速度变化、转向行为和碰撞阶段的结构化动作元组；(4) 该意图条件引导扩散模型生成可执行的对抗轨迹；(5) 采用碰撞引导采样、候选选择和短视界重规划来适应目标车辆动态行为。

Result: 在WOMD-derived闭环场景中，CrashDiffuser单次尝试目标碰撞率为50.33%，三次尝试后为67.98%；接触区域控制成功率为40.05%；轨迹自然度具有竞争力。消融实验验证了各组件设计的有效性。

Conclusion: CrashDiffuser验证了通过VLM语义推理与扩散轨迹生成解耦的架构能够实现细粒度的碰撞位置控制，为安全关键场景生成提供了更精细化的评测手段，对自动驾驶系统的全面安全评估具有重要价值。

Abstract: Generating safety-critical scenarios is essential for evaluating autonomous driving systems. However, existing generators primarily focus on inducing collisions and offer limited control over where contact occurs on the target vehicle. In this paper, we study fine-grained safety-critical scenario generation, where success requires both a target collision and a specified head, rear, or side contact region. We propose CrashDiffuser, a closed-loop VLM-guided diffusion framework that decouples semantic collision reasoning from continuous trajectory synthesis through a hierarchical collision-intent interface derived from the requested target contact region. At initialization, the VLM extracts reusable scene-level context; at each replanning step, it predicts a structured action tuple describing speed change, turning behavior, and collision stage. This intent conditions a diffusion model to generate executable adversarial trajectories, while collision-guided sampling, candidate selection, and short-horizon replanning adapt generation to the target vehicle's evolving behavior. On WOMD-derived closed-loop scenarios, CrashDiffuser achieves a target-collision rate of 50.33% in a single attempt and 67.98% after three attempts, together with a contact-region control success rate of 40.05% and competitive trajectory naturalness. Component ablations further support the proposed design.

</details>


### [337] [Contact-Constrained Lower-Limb Joint-Offset Calibration for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02306)
*Kaixiang Lu,Haiyu Lan,Chunxiao Qiao,You Li,Chengyuan Luo,Enyu Li,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅依赖关节编码器和骨盆IMU的自包含标定框架，用于在静态双足支撑接触下估计人形下肢的编码器零位偏移。通过约束双足固定时足间变换保持恒定，将零位偏移估计转化为12维非线性最小二乘问题。Hessian特征结构分析揭示平行俯仰轴的耦合特性，结合站立姿态膝部先验来锚定弱俯仰链分解。仿真和真机实验验证了该方法的有效性，在A3和A2平台上将足高RMS残差分别从4.26降至2.20 mm和从8.03降至1.43 mm。独立LiDAR-惯性参考验证了俯仰耦合通道的校正效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人下肢的关节编码器零位偏移直接影响运动学一致性，但现有标定方法通常依赖外部动作捕捉系统或标记物，既昂贵又笨重。作者希望开发一种无需外部设备的自包含标定方案，仅利用机载传感器在常规静态站立过程中即可完成编码器偏移的精确估计。

Method: 在双足同时固定的静态支撑条件下，足间变换矩阵由正运动学计算得到必须保持恒定。最小化其姿态依赖性散布构成12维偏移向量上的非线性最小二乘问题。Hessian本征结构分析表明平行俯仰轴会产生旋转耦合，方向残差只能观测俯仰偏移之和；平移和姿态多样性决定剩余数值可观测性。针对A3的pitch-to-roll-to-yaw旋转排序，髋部横滚和偏航激励可降低髋部俯仰耦合，并使用站立姿态膝部先验锚定剩余的弱俯仰链分解。

Result: 仿真和真机注入测试显示偏移恢复一致。在保留录音上，标定将A3足高RMS残差从4.26 mm降至2.20 mm，将A2从8.03 mm降至1.43 mm。独立LiDAR-惯性参考验证俯仰耦合通道：移除注入的俯仰偏移可使腿部里程计的垂直漂移回归至LiDAR轨迹。

Conclusion: 少量静态双足支撑姿态即可为充分激励的方向提供接触一致性的校正，但弱俯仰链中的个别偏移仍依赖先验。该方法的局限性在于：仅靠静态站姿无法完全分离弱耦合通道中的所有俯仰偏移，需要借助膝部先验补充约束。

Abstract: Accurate joint encoder offsets are essential for kinematic consistency in humanoid lower limbs, yet existing calibration methods typically require external motion-capture systems or fiducial targets. We present a self-contained calibration framework exploiting only onboard joint encoders and a pelvis-mounted IMU during static double-support contact. The inter-foot transform from forward kinematics must stay constant when both feet are fixed; minimizing its posture-dependent dispersion yields a nonlinear least-squares problem over the 12-dimensional offset vector. A Hessian eigenstructure analysis shows that parallel pitch axes induce a rotational coupling. Orientation residuals then observe only the pitch-offset sum, while translation and posture diversity set the remaining numerical observability. For the A3 pitch-to-roll-to-yaw ordering, hip-roll and hip-yaw excitation reduce hip-pitch coupling. A standing-posture knee prior then anchors the remaining weak pitch-chain decomposition. Simulation and real-machine injection tests show consistent recovery, and on held-out recordings calibration reduces foot-height RMS residuals from 4.26 to 2.20 mm on A3 and from 8.03 to 1.43 mm on A2. An independent LiDAR-inertial reference checks the pitch-coupled channel. Removing an injected pitch offset moves the leg-odometry vertical drift back toward the LiDAR trajectory. A few static double-support stances thus provide contact-consistent corrections for well-excited directions. Individual offsets in the weak pitch chain remain prior-dependent.

</details>


### [338] [From Multi-Fisheye Sensing to Panoramic Perception: A Parallax-Aware Onboard Platform for Ultra-Low-Altitude UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02319)
*Dun Dai,Ze Lu,Cheng He,Yaowen Wang,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向超低空无人机的视差感知全景平台，将四个同步鱼眼相机流转换为1280x640开放全景图(ERP)，集成于碳纤维机身与Jetson Orin NX上。通过内容自适应接缝搜索和验证门控残差网格等视差感知拼接管线，在50,000+四视图组评估中，精度模式将远场P90特征错位降低41.6%，部署模式以13.29W功耗实现19.99fps实时运行，并在视觉位置识别任务中达到90.8% Recall@5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低空无人机在建筑、植被等障碍物附近飞行时需要环绕视野，但现有方法缺乏兼顾视差感知、嵌入式实时执行和下游视觉接口统一集成的全景感知方案。

Method: 设计专用碳纤维机身集成四颗鱼眼相机、Jetson Orin NX、飞控与GNSS。拼接管线按重叠区域选择投影深度，结合受控接缝与光度融合；提供两种配置：精度模式采用内容自适应接缝搜索及验证门控残差网格，部署模式采用边距门控的每接缝更新以实现传感器速率运行。

Result: 在18个外场序列的50,000余组四视图评估中，精度模式相对固定深度将远场P90特征错位降低41.6%；部署模式在保持最低聚合几何误差的同时，以13.29W功耗稳定运行19.99fps（20Hz回放）；八扇区ERP采样在日间视觉位置识别中达90.8%平均Recall@5。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了集成式机载全景感知架构的有效性，统一了视差感知全景生成、传感器速率嵌入式执行与可复用的下游视觉接口，为超低空无人机提供开源参考实现。

Abstract: Ultra-low-altitude unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) require surround vision near buildings, vegetation, and other obstacles. We present a parallax-aware onboard platform that converts four synchronized fisheye streams into an open 1280x640 equirectangular panorama (ERP) interface. A purpose-built carbon-fiber airframe integrates the cameras, NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX, a flight controller, and a global navigation satellite system (GNSS) receiver. The formation pipeline selects projection depth per overlap and combines controlled seams and photometric fusion. Its accuracy profile adds content-adaptive seam search and a validation-gated residual mesh, whereas its deployed profile retains margin-gated Per-seam updates for sensor-rate operation. Evaluation uses more than 50,000 four-view groups from 18 field sequences. Relative to Fixed Depth, the accuracy profile reduces far-field P90 feature misalignment by 41.6%; the deployed Per-seam profile achieves the lowest aggregate geometric errors across held-out sites. Under a paced 20 Hz replay, the deployed profile sustains 19.99 frames/s at 13.29W mean module-input power. Eight-sector ERP sampling reaches 90.8% mean daytime visual-place-recognition Recall@5. Together, these results validate an integrated onboard panoramic-perception architecture that unifies parallax-aware formation, sensor-rate embedded execution, and reusable downstream vision interfaces for ultra-low-altitude UAVs. The project has been open-sourced at https://github.com/DUNDAI1998/parallax-aware-uav-panorama.

</details>


### [339] [Humanoid Safe Stop via Learned Stoppability Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02358)
*Junfeng Long,Pieter Abbeel,Koushil Sreenath,Roberto Horowitz,Guanya Shi,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Safe-Stop 是一种任务无关的框架，将人形机器人紧急停止重新定义为"可达-规避"(reach-avoid)问题，通过学习停止策略与两个互补的停止可行性估计器，在停止不可行时移交至阻尼坠落回退策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人的紧急停止响应通常执行固定动作，而不考虑当前状态下安全停止是否实际上可行。这种"无脑停车"可能导致危险后果，因此需要对停止可行性进行推理。

Method: Safe-Stop 包含三个核心组件：(1) 学习到的停止策略 (learned stop policy)；(2) 停止概率估计器，由固定停止策略的实际结果监督，捕获学习控制器涌现的停止行为；(3) 可达-规避估计器，由物理状态上的 Hamilton-Jacobi 备份监督，提供互补的可恢复性信号。部署时对两个估计取交集，仅在两者都指示停止可行时才执行停止，否则移交至阻尼坠落回退策略。

Result: 该框架具有跨上游任务的可迁移性（停止策略与估计器不依赖于停止命令前的行为策略，无需重训练即可迁移）；两个估计器的"一致性检查"机制在保持反应速度的同时实现了鲁棒的决策。

Conclusion: 通过将可学习停止策略与互补的监督信号相结合，并以一致性检查作为决策门控，Safe-Stop 为紧急停止提供了更智能、鲁棒且任务无关的解决方案。

Abstract: Humanoid robots responding to emergency stop commands typically execute a fixed maneuver, without reasoning about whether a safe stop is actually feasible from the current state. We cast emergency stopping as a reach-avoid problem and propose Safe-Stop, a task-agnostic framework that pairs a learned stop policy with learned stoppability estimators. The estimators are complementary: a stop-probability estimator supervised by the actual outcomes of the fixed stop policy, and a reach-avoidance estimator supervised by a Hamilton-Jacobi backup over physical state. The first captures emergent stopping behavior of the learned controller; the second provides a complementary recoverability signal. Because the stop policy and estimators do not depend on the behavior policy that preceded the stop command, they transfer across diverse upstream tasks without retraining. At deployment, the two estimates are combined: Safe-Stop commits to the stop only when both estimators indicate that stopping remains feasible, otherwise it hands off to a fall policy, instantiated as a damping fallback. This agreement check yields decisions that are robust without sacrificing reactivity.

</details>


### [340] [A Physics-Consistent Benchmark for Contact-Rich Human-Robot Interaction in Assistive Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02402)
*Chengxiao He,Shanghai Yuan,Liuqun Fan,Shenzhen Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于接触密集型人机交互的物理一致性基准测试，实例化为机器人辅助洗澡任务。该基准包含可变形且被动响应的人体模型、物理感知评分以及冻结的仅视觉/仅评分评估协议。实验表明，任务完成率并不等于物理有效接触，LLM增强状态机在任务成功率达72.9%但物理筛选后降至56.4%，凸显了部署前物理感知筛选的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的任务级评估仅关注机器人策略是否完成指定动作，但在物理人体接触场景中可能遗漏关键的接触失败模式。接触丰富的辅助任务（如辅助洗澡）需要物理响应的人体、超越任务成功的交互质量评估以及无泄漏的观察者-评分者协议，现有评估方法无法满足这些需求。

Method: 提出一个物理一致性基准，具体包括：(1) 可变形、被动响应的仿真人体模型；(2) 结合物理感知评分与任务级成功率的评估体系；(3) 冻结的仅视觉/仅评分评估协议（T1-T7，每种方法140次运行）。物理有效性通过将区域仿真响应与Franka阻抗推压医疗护理人体模型的力量-压痕测量进行校准来建立。

Result: LLM增强状态机（State Machine）任务成功率为72.9%，但经过正确区域和力安全筛选后降至56.4%；VoxPoser产生更轻更稳定的接触但仅完成27.9%试验；零样本pi0.5任务成功率仅0.7%且无正确区域或安全门控成功。

Conclusion: 任务完成本身并不能保证物理有效的接触行为。在部署接触丰富的辅助机器人策略之前，需要引入物理感知的筛选机制来确保接触的正确性和安全性。

Abstract: Conventional task-level evaluation asks whether a robot policy completes a specified action, but can miss failures that emerge only during physical human contact. This limitation is critical in contact-rich assistive tasks, where meaningful evaluation requires a physically responsive human, interaction-quality assessment beyond task success, and a leak-free observer-scorer protocol. We introduce a physics-consistent benchmark for contact-rich human-robot interaction, instantiated in robot-assisted bathing. The benchmark combines a deformable, passively responding human, physics-aware scores alongside task-level success, and a frozen vision-only / scorer-only evaluation protocol. To establish physical validity, region-wise simulated responses are calibrated against force-indentation measurements from Franka impedance pushes on a medical-care manikin. Under a frozen T1-T7 protocol with 140 runs per method, an LLM-augmented state machine (State Machine) achieves 72.9% task success but drops to 56.4% after correct-region and force-safety screening; VoxPoser produces lighter and more stable contact but completes only 27.9% of trials; and zero-shot pi0.5 achieves 0.7% task success with no correct-region or safety-gated successes. These results show that task completion alone does not imply physically valid contact and motivate physics-aware screening before deployment of contact-rich assistive robot policies.

</details>


### [341] [An Adaptive Control Architecture for Slope and Terrain Compensation in Autonomous Navigation in Mediterranean Greenhouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02487)
*Fernando Cañadas-Aránega,Dirk Wollherr,José L. Guzmán,José C. Moreno,José L. Blanco-Claraco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于载荷的地形自适应策略，通过实验表征温室常见土壤类型（混凝土、压实沙和砾石）并使用IMU直接测量地形坡度来估算坡度对电机输入的影响，从而设计由MPC外环和PI内环组成的级联轨迹跟踪方案，配合增益调度的自适应前馈控制来应对坡度与地形类型变化引起的干扰。仿真结果表明差动驱动机器人在误差指标和控制信号效率上均获得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动农业机器人（如温室环境中的机器人）需要在具有不同坡度和质地的地形上稳定移动，微小的地形不规则会导致显著的导航误差，而现有轨迹跟踪方法难以有效应对不同地形条件和坡度变化带来的扰动。

Method: 提出基于载荷的地形自适应策略：(i)对温室最常见的土壤类型（混凝土、压实沙、砾石）进行实验特性表征；(ii)利用IMU直接测量地形坡度，估算坡度角对电机输入的影响力。据此设计级联轨迹跟踪方案，外层采用模型预测控制器（MPC），内层采用PI控制器；通过增益调度的自适应前馈控制来适应坡度和地形类型变化引起的扰动。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方案使差动驱动机器人在误差指标和控制信号效率方面均获得显著提升，验证了所提方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于载荷感知的地形自适应级联控制策略有效提升了移动农业机器人在复杂地形环境下的轨迹跟踪精度与控制效率，为温室等动态农业环境的机器人自主导航提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The ability to move stably over terrain with varying slopes and textures is essential for mobile agricultural robots operating in complex and dynamic environments such as greenhouses, where small terrain irregularities can lead to significant navigation errors. This article presents a novel terrain-adaptation strategy based on the carried payload, ensuring accurate and robust trajectory tracking. The proposed approach is based on: (i) the experimental characterization of the most common types of greenhouse soil, concrete, compacted sand, and gravel, and (ii) the direct measurement of terrain slope using the IMU, in order to estimate the force with which this angle affects the motor input. Based on this information, a cascade trajectory-tracking scheme has been designed, consisting of a model-based predictive controller (MPC) in the outer loop and a PI controller in the inner loop. The system incorporates an adaptive feedforward control through gain scheduling approach, capable of adjusting to disturbances caused by variations in slope and terrain type. Simulation results demonstrate that the differential-drive robot achieves a significant improvement both in error indices and in control signal efficiency, highlighting the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [342] [MS-MEM: Multi-Skill Manipulation-Enhanced Mapping via Uncertainty- and Disturbance-Aware Action Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02493)
*Yitian Shi,Jesper Mücke,Nils Dengler,Sicong Pan,Rania Rayyes,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MS-MEM，一个集成主动视点选择、物体推挤和抓取的多技能操作增强建图框架，用于在货架等拥挤杂乱空间中实现不确定性感知的度量-语义场景建图。通过统一信息增益准则和碰撞扰动约束（CDC），在减少建图不确定性的同时限制场景扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在货架等受限杂乱空间中执行定位和取物任务时，面临严重遮挡、狭窄可达性和避免过度场景变更的需求，现有单技能方法难以同时满足高精度建图和低场景扰动的要求。

Method: 提出MS-MEM框架，包含：1）场景级度量-语义证据信念估计器以建模不确定性；2）创新性的全证据抓取估计器学习抓手可操作性及朝向不确定性，形成不确定性感知抓取表示；3）统一动作选择流程，基于共同信息增益准则评估感知和操作候选动作；4）引入碰撞扰动约束（CDC），惩罚对场景信念中高置信区域造成过度变更的操作，从而在降低建图不确定性的同时限制场景扰动。

Result: 实验表明，与忽略场景扰动的单技能和无约束基线相比，MS-MEM在实现更高建图精度的同时大幅降低场景扰动，验证了主动视点选择、推挤和抓取动作之间的协同增效作用。

Conclusion: MS-MEM通过多技能协同和碰撞扰动约束，有效地在复杂受限环境中平衡了建图精确性与场景变更最小化，展示了一种更具实用价值的主动感知与操作融合范式的可行性。

Abstract: Accurate scene understanding in confined, cluttered spaces such as shelves is essential for service robots, as many everyday tasks require them to locate and retrieve objects reliably. Yet, it remains challenging due to severe occlusions, restricted accessibility, and the need to avoid excessive scene changes. In this paper, we propose Multi-Skill Manipulation-Enhanced Mapping (MS-MEM), an evidential framework for uncertainty-aware mapping that integrates active viewpoint selection, object pushing, and grasping. MS-MEM combines scene-level metric-semantic evidential belief estimators with an uncertainty-aware grasp representation. This representation is learned using a novel full-evidential grasp estimator that models both grasp affordance and orientation uncertainty. In our framework, candidate perception and manipulation actions are evaluated within a unified action selection pipeline using a common information gain criterion. For manipulation actions, we further introduce a collateral disturbance constraint (CDC) that discourages excessive changes to confident regions of the scene belief. This enables MS-MEM to select actions that effectively reduce map uncertainty while limiting collateral scene changes. Experimental results show that, compared with single-skill and unconstrained baselines that ignore scene disturbance, MS-MEM achieves higher mapping accuracy while substantially reducing scene disturbance, highlighting the synergistic effects of active viewpoint selection, push, and grasp actions.

</details>


### [343] [World-Model-Augmented Visual Locomotion for Humanoids on Foothold-Constrained Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02542)
*Yuxi Liu,Lijun Han,Ziming Wang,Ao Zhang,Cong Yang,Wei Sui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WM-LOCO，一种世界模型增强的视觉运动控制方法，通过在PPO策略中联合训练循环世界模型，利用预测性循环特征为足式机器人在稀疏/不连续/受限落脚地形（如踏脚石、缝隙、窄楼梯）上的行走提供前瞻性信息，在仿真和Unitree G1实体机器人上均取得了高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在落脚受限地形（如踏脚石、跨越缝隙、狭窄台阶）上，机器人的每一步落脚都几乎没有容错空间，而完全依赖当前可见地形的策略容易失败。作者希望探究：学习到的近期未来观测与奖励的预测性摘要，能否为这类任务提供所需的预期性信息，从而提升落脚的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出WM-LOCO（World-Model-Augmented Visual Locomotion）框架：联合训练一个循环世界模型和PPO策略。世界模型以本体感知和单张机载深度图像为条件，输出预测性循环特征用于引导策略，且无需显式落脚点标签。

Result: 仿真中，WM-LOCO在缝隙和踏脚石地形上完全成功，而对照基线完全失败；在楼梯上成功率与基线持平，同时提升了步幅效率并降低了骨盆加速度。在物理Unitree G1人形机器人上部署同一策略，使用机载本体感知和单深度流，三种地形平均成功率达93.3%。

Conclusion: 世界模型提供的预测性特征能够为目标落脚受限场景提供所需的预期信息，在无需显式落脚标签的情况下显著提升视觉运动策略在挑战性地形上的成功率与运动质量。

Abstract: Foothold-constrained terrain is characterized by sparse, discontinuous, or geometrically restricted feasible foot contacts, as encountered on stepping stones, across gaps, and on narrow stair treads. On such terrain, a single misstep often leaves little room to recover, so policies that base foot-placement decisions primarily on the immediately visible terrain are prone to failure. We ask whether a learned predictive summary of near-future observations and rewards can provide the anticipatory information required in such settings. We present World-Model-Augmented Visual Locomotion (WM-LOCO), which jointly trains a recurrent world model and a PPO policy. Conditioned on proprioception and a single onboard depth image, the world model produces a predictive recurrent feature that guides the policy, without explicit foothold labels. In simulation, WM-LOCO succeeds on gaps and stepping stones where a matched baseline fails completely, and matches the baseline's success rate on stairs while improving stride efficiency and reducing pelvis acceleration. We deploy the same policy onboard a physical Unitree G1 humanoid using onboard proprioception and a single depth stream; it traverses all three terrain classes with an average success rate of 93.3%.

</details>


### [344] [ZETA: A Controlled Study of Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment VLA Transfer for Tabletop Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02546)
*Mi Yan,Wenhao Zhang,Zhiqi Zhang,Yu Peng,Tangxinyu Wang,Lingfei Zhai,Jiayi Su,Shengliang Deng,Lin Peng,Yaowei Liu,Yuxing Chen,Zhiyuan Wei,Jilong Wang,Jiayi Chen,Jiangran Lyu,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地定义了VLA模型零样本跨实体迁移的两种类型（严格零样本迁移与预训练暴露零样本迁移），并构建了涵盖14个未见目标实体的受控基准，通过系统分析四个关键因素（状态-动作表征、预训练实体多样性、辅助共训练目标、目标实体暴露度），为评估和改进跨实体VLA迁移提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人硬件不断演化且任务数据采集成本高昂，零样本迁移到未见实体对VLA模型至关重要。然而现有文献缺乏统一的零样本迁移定义和受控评估环境，难以将实体变化与任务、场景、协议差异分离开来，阻碍了对该问题的系统性理解。

Method: 首先区分严格零样本迁移（目标实体完全不在训练数据中）和预训练暴露零样本迁移（目标实体仅出现在预训练阶段）。随后构建包含14个未见目标实体的受控基准，覆盖仿真与真实世界验证。在该框架内对四个因素进行受控分析：状态-动作表征、预训练实体多样性、辅助共训练目标、目标实体暴露度。

Result: 局部末端执行器（EEF）状态-动作表征、源实体多样性和辅助共训练分别使跨实体迁移性能提升约15、18和7个百分点；预训练中仅添加5%的目标实体数据即可使目标实体平均进度提升13.4个百分点，表明严格与预训练暴露零样本迁移存在本质差异需分别报告。

Conclusion: 研究为桌面双指夹持静态操作的跨实体VLA迁移评估与改进提供了实用指导，同时为未来在移动基座控制、灵巧手和长时程任务等更广泛场景中的探索奠定了基础。

Abstract: Zero-shot generalization to unseen embodiments is important for generalizable vision-language-action (VLA) models as robot hardware evolves and task-specific data collection remains costly. However, a systematic understanding of this problem remains limited, in part because the literature lacks a unified zero-shot transfer definition and controlled evaluation settings that isolate embodiment changes from differences in tasks, scenes, or protocols. To address this gap, we first distinguish strict zero-shot transfer, where the target embodiment is absent from all training data, from pretrain-exposed zero-shot transfer, where it appears only during pretraining. We then introduce a controlled benchmark spanning 14 held-out target embodiments across simulation and real-world validation. Within this framework, we conduct a controlled analysis of four factors: state-action representations, pretraining embodiment diversity, auxiliary co-training objectives, and target-embodiment exposure. Experimental results show that local end-effector (EEF) state-action representations, the source embodiment diversity, and auxiliary co-training improve cross-embodiment transfer by around 15, 18, and 7 percentage points, respectively. We further find that adding only 5% target-embodiment data during pretraining improves average target-embodiment progress by 13.4 percentage points, showing that strict and pretrain-exposed zero-shot transfer are distinct and should be reported separately. Together, these findings provide practical guidance for evaluating and improving cross-embodiment VLA transfer in stationary tabletop manipulation with two-finger grippers, while motivating future investigation of broader settings including mobile-base control, dexterous hands, and long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [345] [Pre-Lane-change Signal in Transitional Autonomous Vehicles: Results from Controlled Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02575)
*Zeyu Mu,Danjue Chen,Abhinav Sharma,George F. List*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了量产过渡型自动驾驶车辆（tAV）在强制变道中的决策与执行过程。基于NC-tALC实验中150次受控强制变道数据，定义信号时间（SigT）为变道侧移前的运行参考点，利用Firth逻辑回归预测tAV最终选择前方或后方目标间隙，并分别分析就位与重新定位两种情况下的纵向行进轨迹。模型五折交叉验证平均准确率达0.89，支持"纵向准备+横向执行"两阶段变道过程猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶变道研究多聚焦于完整变道轨迹或横向执行阶段，缺乏对变道开始前纵向准备阶段的理解。作者旨在探究目标间隙是否在侧移开始前可观测，以及tAV如何从变道前状态纵向推进至变道起始点，以填补对强制变道早期决策过程的认识空白。

Method: 研究基于NC-tALC实验中150次受控强制变道场景；定义SigT作为变道侧移开始前的运行参考点；使用Firth逻辑回归，以SigT时刻的相对位置和相对速度预测tAV最终并入最近目标车道车辆的前方或后方；将纵向推进过程区分为"就位"（in-position）与"重新定位"（repositioning）两种情形分别分析；通过五折交叉验证评估模型性能。

Result: SigT时刻的交通状态包含关于最终目标间隙选择的大量信息，且能提供从纵向准备到侧移开始前的有效提前时间。模型平均五折交叉验证准确率达0.89，能预测tAV是保持当前间隙还是通过前进或后退重新定位至邻近间隙，即使存在纵向重叠或当前间隙几何关系模糊的情况。初步证据表明，就位与重新定位情形从SigT到变道起始点遵循不同的纵向路径。

Conclusion: 研究支持可观测变道过程的两阶段猜想：第一阶段为从SigT到变道起始点的纵向准备，第二阶段为横向机动执行。该框架适用于就位、重新定位及纵向重叠情形，可支持区分目标间隙选择与横向启动时机、并表征侧移前纵向准备的变道建模方法。

Abstract: This paper investigates how a production transitional autonomous vehicle (tAV) develops and executes mandatory lane-change decisions. Using 150 controlled mandatory lane changes from the NC-tALC experiments, the study examines whether the eventual target gap is observable before lateral movement begins and how the tAV progresses longitudinally from that pre-lane-change state to lane-change start.
  Signal time (SigT) is defined as an operational pre-lane-change-start reference point. A Firth logistic regression predicts whether the tAV eventually merges in front of or behind its nearest target-lane vehicle using relative position and relative speed at SigT. Longitudinal progression from SigT to lane-change start is then examined separately for in-position and repositioning cases.
  The traffic state at SigT contains substantial information about eventual target-gap choice and provides meaningful lead time before lateral movement begins. The proposed formulation predicts whether the tAV remains with its current gap or repositions to a neighboring gap by moving forward or dropping back, including cases with longitudinal overlap and ambiguous current-gap geometry. The model achieves an average five-fold cross-validated accuracy of 0.89. Results also provide preliminary evidence that in-position and repositioning cases follow different longitudinal pathways from SigT to lane-change start.
  These findings support a two-stage conjecture of the observable lane-change process: longitudinal preparation from SigT to lane-change start, followed by lateral maneuver execution. The formulation applies to in-position, repositioning, and longitudinally overlapping cases, and can support lane-change models that distinguish target-gap choice from lateral-onset timing while representing longitudinal preparation before lateral movement begins.

</details>


### [346] [Advancing Accessible Underwater Robotics: The Mini-Girona I-AUV at RAMI 2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02605)
*Taqi Hamoda,Bilal Ahmed,Deborah Ele-Ojo,Thi Tran Ha Bao,Adel Saidani,Mazen Elgabalawy,Alaaeddine Chaarani,Sebastian Realpe,Patryk Cieslak,Pere Ridao,Narcis Palomeras,Nuno Gracias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Mini-Girona干预型自主水下机器人（I-AUV）的设计与开发。该平台造价约5万美元，配备了5自由度机械臂、立体视觉和AI处理能力，旨在以亲民价格弥合昂贵研究级AUV与基础ROV之间的差距。在RAMI 2025学生竞赛中，尽管面临热管理和团队访问受限等挑战，仍获得了总成绩第二名，尤其在视觉感知和干预任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究级AUV成本高昂、专业性过强，而基础ROV功能有限，存在明显的技术断层。作者希望开发一款价格适中且易于使用的水下机器人平台，以推动水下机器人研究和教育的普及。

Method: 设计了Mini-Girona I-AUV平台，集成5自由度机械臂、立体视觉系统和AI驱动处理模块，支持自主导航与干预任务；随后通过参加RAMI 2025学生竞赛对平台性能进行实证评估。

Result: Mini-Girona在RAMI 2025学生竞赛中获得总排名第二，在基于视觉的感知和干预任务中表现优异；热管理问题和团队访问限制被识别为需要改进的关键短板。

Conclusion: Mini-Girona展示了作为经济可行的水下机器人研究与教育平台的价值，能够在真实世界水下应用场景中激发创新，为该领域提供了高性价比的替代方案。

Abstract: The Mini-Girona Intervention Autonomous Underwater Vehicle (I-AUV) represents an advancement in accessible underwater robotics, designed to bridge the gap between costly, specialized research AUVs and basic Remotely Operated Vehicles (ROVs). Developed with a focus on affordability and usability, the Mini-Girona, priced at approximately $50,000, integrates advanced components such as a 5-DOF manipulator arm, stereo vision, and AI-driven processing for autonomous navigation and intervention tasks. This paper presents the design and development of the Mini-Girona, detailing its performance during the RAMI 2025 student competition. Despite challenges such as thermal management issues and restricted team access, the Mini-Girona achieved second place overall, excelling in vision-based perception and intervention tasks. This work highlights the platform's potential as a tool for underwater robotics research and education, fostering innovation in real-world underwater applications.

</details>


### [347] [Latent Cluster Analysis for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02634)
*Theodor Wulff,Sergio Lanza,Tamara Bila,Angelo Cangelosi,Stefan Wermter,Igor Farkas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出LAVLA框架，对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型GR00T N1.5进行逐层潜在聚类分析，通过交叉注意力加权方法提升聚类效果，并提取可解释概念，揭示潜在空间中新时空与运动学特征逐层解耦的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人领域的应用日益广泛，但其内部表征如何驱动行为仍缺乏深入理解，本文旨在填补这一可解释性空白。

Method: 提出LAVLA潜在聚类分析框架，对GR00T N1.5进行逐层分析；引入基于交叉注意力的嵌入加权方法，放大相关特征并抑制无关特征；为每个聚类提取人类可理解的概念。

Result: 加权聚类在定量评估中一致优于基线；潜在聚类在中间层逐步解耦新时空与运动学特征，表征在中间层更精细并向输出层趋于稳定。

Conclusion: LAVLA推进了语言驱动的机器人系统的可解释性研究。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) Models are increasingly used in robotics for their ability to ground language and perception into action, yet the internal representations driving their behaviour remain poorly understood. We propose LAVLA, a framework for latent cluster analysis of VLA models, and conduct a layer-wise study of the state-of-the-art GR00T N1.5 model, with particular focus on its action decoder. To better characterise the latent space during action diffusion, we introduce a cross-attention-based embedding-weighting method that amplifies relevant features while suppressing less informative ones. Quantitative evaluation shows that weighted clustering consistently outperforms the baseline. To improve interpretability, we extract human-interpretable concepts for each cluster, linking latent representations to semantic descriptions. Our analysis shows that latent clusters progressively disentangle spatiotemporal and kinematic features, with representations becoming more refined in the middle layers and stabilising toward the output. As such, LAVLA advances the interpretability of language-driven robotic systems.

</details>


### [348] [HINT: Human-Intent Inception for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02653)
*Mingyu Mei,Haojie Xu,Shihao Jin,Zibo Dai,Qihao Cheng,Zhengrui Lv,Hongjie Fang,Shirun Tang,Guang Chen,Xinyue Zhao,Huiliang Shen,Zaixing He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HINT是一个受人类操控原理启发的智能体框架，仅在操控模式转换时调用语义推理以解析当前子任务和目标，然后通过多视图锚定与视觉跟踪维持这一意图承诺，并以图像空间语义高亮和注意力先验注入两种视觉接口将跟踪到的意图传递给动作策略，无需额外训练参数即可显著提升长时程任务的意图理解与操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言动作（VLA）模型和其他动作策略难以在密集演化的视觉输入与稀疏语言指导下实现高层级智能行为。视觉相关性往往会主导语义意图，导致动作跟随视觉捷径而非人类目标，因此需要一种能将稀疏语义意图与密集连续控制解耦的框架。

Method: HINT遵循人类操控的两条原则：语义意图在操控模式转换处稀疏变化，而连续控制主要依赖演化的手-物关系。框架在模式转换时刻才调用语义推理来解析当前子任务和目标，随后通过多视图锚定和视觉跟踪维持该意图承诺。设计了两种视觉接口——图像空间语义高亮和注意力先验注入——向动作策略传递跟踪到的意图，无需在基础动作模型中引入额外可训练参数。

Result: 在三个长时程任务及其分布外变体上的实验表明，HINT在两种基础策略上均显著提升了意图理解、任务进度和端到端成功率，同时保持了低延迟控制。

Conclusion: 通过将稀疏语义推理与密集连续控制解耦，HINT有效克服了视觉捷径问题，实现了更接近人类的高层级操控智能，且无需修改基础动作模型的结构。

Abstract: Humans can perform complex manipulations given a simple intent through an overall instruction, while continuously adapting to evolving visual observations. However, current vision-language action (VLA) models and other action policies struggle to realize this high-level intelligent behavior under dense, evolving visual inputs and sparse language guidance. Visual correlations can then dominate semantic intent, leading actions to follow visual shortcuts rather than human goals. We present HINT (Human-INTent INcepTion), an agentic framework inspired by the human manipulation principles: semantic intent changes sparsely at manipulation-pattern transitions, whereas continuous control primarily depends on the evolving object-hand relationship. HINT invokes semantic reasoning only at pattern transitions to resolve the current subtask and target, then maintains this commitment through multi-view grounding and visual tracking. We explore two visual interfaces-image-space semantic highlighting and attention-prior injection-to communicate the tracked intent to the action policy without introducing additional trainable parameters into the foundation action model. Experiments across three long-horizon tasks and out-of-distribution variants show that HINT substantially improves intent understanding, task progress, and end-to-end success across two foundation policies while preserving low-latency control.

</details>


### [349] [From Proxy Learning to Driving Decisions: A Transfer-Based Framework for Evaluating Future-Aware Autonomous Driving Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02688)
*Yikai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出代理到决策迁移（PDT）框架，用于评估在自动驾驶规划中，学习到的未来感知表示（如世界模型）是否真正带来了可靠的行车性能提升，而不仅仅是在代理指标或受限子集上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有未来感知/世界模型越来越多地被用于基于提议的自动驾驶规划器，但研究往往把代理目标或受限子集上的改进误当作规划增益，未验证提议排序、所选轨迹、全尺度效用及关键驾驶组件是否真正受益。

Method: 提出PDT框架，包含两个核心模块：（1）决策-迁移分解模块，通过评分间隔（score margins）、切换条件效用（switch-conditioned utility）和支撑-选择遗憾（support-versus-selection regret）定位价值损失；（2）可靠性约束验证模块，要求精确配对、最小有意义效应、扩大尺度确认、安全不补偿、顺序可比性和族级稳健性。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v1上的代表性未来感知规划器中，组件BCE从0.705降至0.530，但保留的所选PDM从0.963降至0.961。一个候选在512条记录前缀上提升0.00909，场景自举95%置信区间为[0.000744, 0.0177]，但其2048条记录和完整支持区间包含零。提议级重放确认了切换效用分解，但432个筛选配置中没有一个通过两半两种子稳健性门槛。

Conclusion: PDT框架识别了决策迁移在代理、子集、聚合和选择证据层面失败或无法确定的具体位置，说明单纯依赖代理或局部指标可能高估未来感知表示的实际规划收益。

Abstract: Future-aware representations and world models are increasingly used in proposal-based autonomous-driving planners to improve trajectory selection. However, improvements in proxy objectives or restricted subsets are often interpreted as planning gains without verifying proposal ordering, selected trajectories, full-scale utility, and critical driving components. We propose the Proxy-to-Decision Transfer (PDT) Framework, an analysis framework that evaluates when learned future information supports a reliable driving-performance improvement claim. Its Decision-Transfer Decomposition Module localizes value loss through score margins, switch-conditioned utility, and support-versus-selection regret. Its Reliability-Constrained Validation Module requires exact pairing, a minimum meaningful effect, scale-expanded confirmation, safety non-compensation, sequential comparability, and family-level robustness. On a representative future-aware planner evaluated with NAVSIM-v1, component BCE decreases from 0.705 to 0.530 while held selected PDM decreases from 0.963 to 0.961. A separate candidate improves a 512-record prefix by 0.00909, with a scene-bootstrap 95% interval of [0.000744, 0.0177], but its 2048-record and complete-support intervals include zero. A proposal-level replay further confirms the switch-utility decomposition, yet none of 432 screened configurations passes the two-half, two-seed robustness gate. PDT therefore identifies where decision transfer fails or remains indeterminate across proxy, subset, aggregate, and selection evidence.

</details>


### [350] [Do Better Imagined Rollouts Mean Better Robot Control? A Controlled Study of World-Model Evaluation Under Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02811)
*Dharini Raghavan,Amritpal Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人预测模型在开环与闭环评估之间的差异。在差速驱动路径跟踪任务中（存在有偏里程计和间歇性路标感知），对六种状态估计器在24种感知条件下使用轨迹重放、20步无测量展开和闭环跟踪三种方式评估。结果表明轨迹重放的定位RMSE与闭环横向误差相关性更强（Spearman rho=0.923 vs 0.774），并在5/24条件下选出与闭环最优不同的估计器（远少于展开指标的18/24）。展开预测步长和无测量间隔会产生不同的排名一致性；针对更长感知中断训练的循环估计器在联合感知退化下可改善EKF锚定模型，但增益在孤立中断或不同架构间不一致。结论是机器人预测模型评估应同时明确预测视界和测量更新调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测模型在机器人领域被广泛用于状态估计、规划、控制及策略评估，但通常仅以固定视界的开环预测精度来评判。然而在实际闭环运行中，机器人会反复执行动作、接收新测量、更新状态估计并重算控制，这种开环与闭环的差距被低估，需要系统研究二者的关系。

Method: 在差速驱动机器人的路径跟踪任务中设置有偏里程计与间歇性路标感知条件。评估六种状态估计器（包含传统估计器与循环神经网络等递归估计器）在24种感知条件下的表现。采用三种评估方式：轨迹重放、20步无测量展开预测、真实闭环跟踪。进一步系统变化预测视界（H）和测量更新间隔，并测试针对更长感知中断训练的递归估计器的效果，通过Spearman秩相关系数等指标比较各评估方式对真实闭环性能的预测能力。

Result: 轨迹重放的定位RMSE与闭环横向RMSE相关性比展开误差更强（rho=0.923 vs 0.774），在5/24条件下选出与闭环最优不同的估计器，而展开指标在18/24条件下选错。当H=20时，每次都有测量的排名一致性为rho=0.916，无测量时降至rho=0.774。视界-更新网格显示，保留常规校正时长的预测视界仍具有信息量，而无校正的长展开会产生与闭环行为显著不同的排名。针对更长感知中断的训练使GRU-EKF在联合感知退化下交叉轨道RMSE从1.72m降至1.06m，但在孤立中断或不同架构下增益不一致。

Conclusion: 机器人预测模型的评估应同时明确预测视界和测量更新调度这两个关键参数。对用于反馈控制的模型，离线展开只有在感知与校正模式反映闭环操作时才最具信息价值。研究结果为选择与真实闭环性能最对齐的离线评估协议提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Predictive models are increasingly used in robotics for state estimation, planning, control, and policy evaluation, yet they are often judged by open-loop prediction accuracy over a fixed horizon. In closed-loop operation, a robot repeatedly acts, receives new measurements, updates its state estimate, and recomputes control. We study this difference in a differential-drive path-tracking task with biased odometry and intermittent landmark sensing. Six state estimators are evaluated across 24 sensing conditions using trajectory replay, a 20-step measurement-free rollout, and closed-loop tracking. Replay position RMSE correlates more strongly with closed-loop cross-track RMSE than rollout error (Spearman rho = 0.923 vs. 0.774) and selects a different estimator from the closed-loop optimum in 5/24 conditions, compared with 18/24 for the rollout metric. We then vary rollout horizon and measurement-update interval. With H=20, rank agreement decreases from rho = 0.916 with measurements at every step to rho = 0.774 with no measurements. A horizon-update grid shows that long prediction horizons remain informative when regular corrections are retained, whereas long rollouts without correction can produce rankings that differ substantially from closed-loop behavior. We also test recurrent estimators trained on longer sensing outages. This improves the EKF-anchored models under combined sensing degradation, reducing GRU-EKF cross-track RMSE from 1.72 m to 1.06 m, but the gain is not consistent across isolated outages or estimator architectures. These results show that predictive-model evaluation in robotics should specify both prediction horizon and measurement-update schedule. For models used in feedback, offline rollouts are most informative when their sensing and correction pattern reflects closed-loop operation. Code is available at https://github.com/rdharini2001/Robot_World_Model

</details>


### [351] [Toward Robust LiDAR Semantic Segmentation for Real-World Deployment: Evaluation under Coarse Labels, Adverse Conditions, and Domain Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02830)
*Samir Abou Haidar,Alexandre Chariot,Mehdi Darouich,Cyril Joly,Jean-Emmanuel Deschaud*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套结构化的评估协议，从粗粒度安全标签、八种LiDAR退化场景鲁棒性以及跨数据集域泛化三个维度，评估LiDAR语义分割模型的部署就绪度，并在嵌入式Jetson AGX Orin平台上测量推理速度。结果表明：细粒度基准排名不能反映安全相关表现，所有方法在退化条件下都有显著性能下降，且当前域泛化能力不足以支持可靠部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR语义分割评估协议仅聚焦于干净、单领域环境下的细粒度标签分类，未同时覆盖安全关键标签语义、传感退化条件和跨域变化三个方面的部署就绪度评估，导致模型实际部署能力缺乏系统验证。

Method: 提出三合一评估协议：(i) 基于自动驾驶安全优先级的粗粒度标签评估；(ii) 八类LiDAR退化（模拟大气、几何和传感退化）下的鲁棒性测试；(iii) 无适应跨数据集域泛化评估。同时在嵌入式Jetson AGX Orin平台测量推理速度以反映真实部署约束。

Result: 发现细粒度基准排名并不总能反映安全相关性能；所有方法在退化条件下均出现显著性能下降且鲁棒性特征与网络架构相关；当前域泛化能力不足以支撑可靠部署，揭示了基准表现与部署就绪度之间的具体差距。

Conclusion: 本文暴露了基准测试性能与实际部署就绪度之间的鸿沟，为LiDAR语义分割提供了更贴近实际应用的参考评估协议。

Abstract: LiDAR-based semantic segmentation is a core perception module for autonomous vehicles and mobile robots. Despite the strong performance of recent state-of-the-art methods on standard benchmarks, existing evaluation protocols remain focused on clean, single-domain settings and fine-grained label taxonomies, leaving deployment readiness largely unassessed. Real-world systems must handle safety-critical label semantics, degraded sensing conditions, and cross-domain variability, yet no unified protocol currently addresses all three aspects together. In this paper, we propose a structured evaluation protocol that assesses the deployment readiness of LiDAR semantic segmentation models along three complementary dimensions: (i) coarse-label evaluation aligned with autonomous driving safety priorities, revealing how label granularity affects different methods; (ii) robustness under eight types of LiDAR corruptions designed to emulate real-world atmospheric, geometric, and sensor degradations; and (iii) domain generalization across datasets without adaptation. The evaluation includes inference speed measured on an embedded Jetson AGX Orin platform, directly reflecting deployment constraints. Our results show that fine-grained benchmark rankings do not always reflect safety-relevant performance, that all methods experience substantial degradation under corruptions with architecture-dependent robustness characteristics, and that current domain generalization remains insufficient for reliable deployment. These findings expose concrete gaps between benchmark performance and deployment readiness, and provide a reference protocol for more practically grounded evaluation of LiDAR semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [352] [Towards Trustworthy Autonomous Robots: An Explainable AI-Based Decision Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02861)
*Cagri Temel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，通过四层可审计架构（语义感知、信念推理、动作合成、执行验证），确保自主机器人的每个决策都能通过文档化的因果链追溯到传感器证据，解决了深度学习驱动的自主系统在事故调查中的可审计性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于深度学习的自主机器人在发生事故时面临根本性的可审计性挑战——调查人员无法重建系统做出特定决策的原因。现有事后可解释方法（如LIME）虽能提供特征归因，但缺乏决策级重建所需的工件结构。

Method: TRACE将决策过程组织为四个可审计层：语义感知（基于证据的实体识别）、信念推理（基于因果图的概率状态估计）、动作合成（带反事实文档的约束感知规划）、执行验证（合规监控）。框架与模型无关，可集成CNN和Transformer等学习型感知模块，同时保持决策级可审计性。

Result: 在仓库机器人导航的500次模拟决策循环实验中，TRACE实现了98.6%的证据可追溯性、99.0%的时间连续性和98.1%的决策可重建性。

Conclusion: TRACE满足了EU AI Act对高风险系统透明度的要求，为安全关键型自主系统推动了可解释AI的发展。

Abstract: Autonomous robots powered by deep learning face a fundamental auditability challenge: when incidents occur, investigators cannot reconstruct why the system made specific decisions. This paper presents TRACE (Transparent Reasoning Architecture for Credible Execution), a decision framework that ensures every autonomous action can be traced back to sensor evidence through documented causal chains. The framework organizes decision-making into four auditable layers: Semantic Perception for evidence-grounded entity recognition, Belief Reasoning for probabilistic state estimation with causal graphs, Action Synthesis for constraint-aware planning with counterfactual documentation, and Execution Verification for compliance monitoring. TRACE is model-agnostic yet designed to integrate learning-based perception modules (CNNs, transformers) while preserving decision-level auditability. We evaluate the framework using three objective metrics: Evidence Traceability (sensor-to-decision linkage), Decision Reconstructability (post-hoc analysis capability), and Temporal Continuity (audit trail completeness). Experimental evaluation on warehouse robot navigation demonstrates that TRACE achieves 98.6% evidence traceability, 99.0% temporal continuity, and 98.1% decision reconstructability across 500 simulated decision cycles. Post-hoc methods like LIME provide feature attributions but lack the artifact structure needed for decision-level reconstruction. The framework addresses EU AI Act requirements for high-risk system transparency and contributes to Explainable AI for safety-critical autonomous systems.

</details>
